    this.format='PNG';this.colorDepth='8-bit';
    this.camera='Active View';this.focalLength=50;this.sensorWidth=36;
    this.fStop=8;this.iso=100;

    this.cpuThreads='Auto';this.tileSize='Auto';this.acceleration='BVH';
    this.lightTree=false;this.pathGuiding=false;
  }
  clone(){return Object.freeze({...this});}
}

}

class RenderJob{
  constructor(id,settings,mode='full',region=null){
    this.id=id;
    this.settings=settings;
    this.mode=mode;
    this.region=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.cancelled=false;this.paused=false;this.finished=false;this.error=null;
    this.startedAt=0;this.finishedAt=0;this.renderStartedAt=0;
    this.progress=new RenderProgressState();
    this.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    this.controlToken16=new RenderControlToken16();
    this.setCancelled=v=>{this.cancelled=!!v;if(v)this.controlToken16.cancel();};
    this.setPaused=v=>{this.paused=!!v;v?this.controlToken16.pause():this.controlToken16.resume();};
  }
}

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

var RenderPerformanceMetrics=Object.freeze({
  begin(job){job.startedAt=performance.now();job.finishedAt=0;return job.startedAt;},
  beginTracing(job){job.renderStartedAt=performance.now();},
  finish(job){
    job.finishedAt=performance.now();
    job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,job.finishedAt-(job.startedAt||job.finishedAt));
    const seconds=Math.max(1e-6,(job.finishedAt-(job.renderStartedAt||job.startedAt||job.finishedAt))/1000);
    const measured=RenderCoreH.performance(job);
    const actualRays=Math.max(0,Number(measured?.totalRays)||0);
    job.progress.raysPerSecond=actualRays?Math.round(actualRays/seconds):0;
    job.performance=Object.freeze({
      totalMs:job.progress.elapsedMs,
      traceMs:seconds*1000,
      actualRays,
      raysPerSecond:job.progress.raysPerSecond,
      samples:Number(measured?.samples)||0,
      yields:Number(measured?.yields)||0,
      bvhBuildMs:job.progress.bvhBuildMs
    });
    return job.performance;
  }
});

class RenderJobController{
  constructor(onProgress){
    this.activeJob=null;
    this.lastJob=null;
    this.onProgress=onProgress||(()=>{});
    this.workerPool=new RenderWorkerPool();
    this.generation=0;
  }

  configureThreads(setting){
    return this.workerPool.setThreadSetting(setting);
  }

  workerStats(){
    return this.workerPool.stats();
  }

  start(job){
    if(this.activeJob&&!this.activeJob.finished&&!this.activeJob.cancelled)this.cancel(false);
    this.configureThreads(job?.settings?.cpuThreads||'Auto');
    this.activeJob=job;
    this.lastJob=job;
    RenderPerformanceMetrics.begin(job);
    const generation=++this.generation;

    job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.percent=0;
    this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

    this._prepare(job,generation);
    return job;
  }

  async _prepare(job,generation){
    try{
      if(!job.renderScene)throw new Error('Render job missing RenderSceneSnapshot');

      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=this.workerPool.available;
      job.progress.threads=this.workerPool.size;
      const prepared=await RendererMasterCore.prepare(job,this.workerPool,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=Math.max(0,Math.min(95,Math.round(ratio*95)));
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });
      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
      job.executionGeometry=prepared.geometry;job.preparedGeometry=prepared.prepared;job.acceleration=prepared.acceleration;job.shadingContext=prepared.shading;job.emissiveTriangles=prepared.shading?.emissiveTriangles||null;
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.percent=96;this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`
        : 'Direct';
      const activeRenderer=RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job);
      const diagnostic=await activeRenderer.render(job,job.acceleration,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        if(job.previewFrame){
          try{RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(job.previewFrame);RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();}catch(_){}
        }
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.beautyFrame=diagnostic;
      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
    }else if(region){

      if(!region)return false;
    }
    if(RendererRuntimeState.degraded){

        typeof RendererMasterCore?.createRequest==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.prepare==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.rendererFor==='function' &&
        !!this.jobs &&
        !!this.ui?.frameBuffer;

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        RendererBootstrap.showFailure(job.error);
        return job;
      }
    }
    try{

      job=RendererMasterCore.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'snapshot',snapshotStart16);

    }catch(err){

      job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'snapshot',job.error);

      if(notice)notice.innerHTML='Render snapshot failed.<br>'+String(err?.message||err);
      console.error('3DLite Render snapshot:',err);
      return job;
    }

    this.lastRegion=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.resize(...job.progress.resolution);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice)notice.innerHTML=`Render snapshot ready.<br>${job.renderScene.objects.length} object(s) • ${job.renderScene.materials.length} material(s).`;
    this.jobs.start(job);
    return job;
  },

  renderLast(){
    const settings=this.lastCompletedSettings||this.lastSettings;
    const region=this.lastCompletedSettings?this.lastCompletedRegion:this.lastRegion;
    return settings?this.render('last',region,settings):this.render();
  },

  mapDisplayedRegion(localRegion){
    return RenderRegion14.mapNested(this.lastCompletedRegion,localRegion);
  },

  renderRegion(region=null){
    if(region){
      const clean=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!clean)return false;
      this.pendingRegion=clean;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
      return this.render('region',clean);
    }

    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    if(this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame){
      this.regionSelectArmed=true;
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        notice.style.display='flex';
        notice.innerHTML='Render Region: drag on the render image to choose an area.';
      }
      return true;
    }

    const fallback=Object.freeze({x:.25,y:.25,width:.5,height:.5});
    this.pendingRegion=fallback;
    return this.render('region',fallback);
  },

  recordCompleted(job){
    if(!job||job.cancelled||job.error)return false;
    this.lastCompletedSettings=job.settings||null;
    this.lastCompletedRegion=RenderRegion14.sanitize(job.region);
    return true;
  },

  stop(){return this.jobs.cancel(true);},
  pause(){return this.jobs.pause();},
  resume(){return this.jobs.resume();},
  restart(){
    const settings=this.lastSettings||this.readSettings();
    return this.render('restart',this.lastRegion,settings);
  },

  showSettings(){this.ui.open(this.ui.settingsWindow);},
  showFrameBuffer(){this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();this.updateFrameActions();},

  updateFrameActions(){
    const valid=!!this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame;
    for(const id of ['rfSave','rfCopy']){
      const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.disabled=!valid;
    }
    return valid;
  },

var RenderTileScheduler16=Object.freeze({
  makeTiles(width,height,tileSize){
    const size=Math.max(4,Math.min(128,Number(tileSize)||16));
    const tiles=[];
    let id=0;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y+=size){
      for(let x=0;x<width;x+=size){
        tiles.push(Object.freeze({
          id:id++,x,y,
          width:Math.min(size,width-x),
          height:Math.min(size,height-y)
        }));
      }
    }
    return tiles;
  },
  async runLocal(job,tiles,runTile,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const total=Math.max(1,tiles.length);
    for(let i=0;i<tiles.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      await runTile(tiles[i],i);
      onProgress((i+1)/total);
      if((i&1)===1)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
  }
});
window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;

    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const compiled=RenderPart4Core.compile(job);
    const backgroundFallback=RenderColor.hexToLinear(job.settings.background||'#202020');
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const primaryBatch16=new RenderRayBatch16(Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)));
    const rowChunk=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));
    await RenderTileScheduler16.runLocal(job,tiles16,async tile=>{
      for(let y=tile.y;y<tile.y+tile.height;y++){
        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const mb=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(metallic,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(mb.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Metallic BRDF test failed');

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);
  if(tm.some(x=>x<0||x>1||!Number.isFinite(x)))errors.push('Tone mapping range test failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  <div id="importDialogBackdrop" class="importDialogBackdrop" aria-hidden="true">
    <div class="importDialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="importDialogTitle">

            <option value="all">All Supported Types (*.obj; *.fbx)</option>

          </select>

        <div id="importTypeInfo" class="small" style="margin:6px 0 10px">Shows all supported 3D object files.</div>

        <button id="chooseImportFileBtn" type="button">Choose File…</button>
        <div id="importChosenFile" class="importChosenFile">No file selected</div>

              <label><input type="radio" name="importFileCount" value="multiple"> Multiple Objects</label>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="importGroup">
            <div class="importGroupTitle">Import As</div>
            <div class="importRadioRow">
              <label><input type="radio" name="importAs" value="editable" checked> Editable Poly</label>
              <label><input type="radio" name="importAs" value="mesh"> Mesh Object</label>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="importGroup">
            <div class="importGroupTitle">Mesh Structure</div>
            <div class="importRadioRow" style="display:grid;gap:5px">
              <label><input type="radio" name="imp
--- occurrence 3 at 368947 ---
(0,.2126*env[0]+.7152*env[1]+.0722*env[2]);
          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;
      }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environment energy is accounted for by path miss evaluation.
      const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(
        job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed^(bounce*0x9E3779B9)
      );
      const safeDirect=this.safeColor(direct);
      radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,safeDirect));

      if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
        const w=Math.max(0,.2126*safeDirect[0]+.7152*safeDirect[1]+.0722*safeDirect[2]);
        job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
      }

      if(bounce>=1){
        if(job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='None')break;
        if(LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)){
          const cachedTail=LightCacheEstimator.estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,pixelSeed,bounce);
          if(cachedTail){
            radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,cachedTail));
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if(material.shading==='unlit')break;

      const sample=GuidedBSDFSampler.sample(job,material,n,v,hit.position,pixelSeed,bounce);
      if(!sample)break;

      throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,sample.weight);

      if(!this.finiteColor(throughput))break;
      if(Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])<1e-6)break;

      // Russian roulette after the first few bounces. Survival probability tracks path throughput.
      if(bounce>=2){
        const survive=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.95,Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])));
        if(PathSampler.scalar(pixelSeed,bounce,15)>survive)break;
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,1/survive);
      }

      if(sample.transmission&&material.thickness>0){
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(
          throughput,
          DielectricTransport.beerLambert(material.attenuationColor,material.thickness,material.attenuationDistance)
        );
      }

      previousGuide=(!sample.transmission&&job.pathGuide&&RayMath.dot(n,sample.direction)>0)
        ? {position:hit.position.slice(),normal:n.slice(),direction:sample.direction.slice()}
        : null;

      const origin=this.offsetOrigin(hit,n,sample.direction);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const rayType=sample.transmission?RenderRayType.TRANSMISSION:(sample.lobe==='ggx'?RenderRayType.GLOSSY:RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
      RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job).addRay(rayType);
      ray=new RenderRay(origin,sample.direction,eps,Infinity,rayType,bounce+1);
    }

    return radiance;
  },

  tracePixel(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed){
    const primaryHitRecord=acceleration.trace(primaryRay);
    if(!primaryHitRecord.hit){
      const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,primaryRay.direction));
      return {radiance:env,primaryHit:false,primaryHitRecord:null};
    }
    return {
      radiance:this.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,primaryHitRecord),
      primaryHit:true,
      primaryHitRecord
    };
  }
});

class ProgressiveAccumulator{
  constructor(width,height){
    this.width=width;this.height=height;
    const n=width*height;
    this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
    this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
    this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
    this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
    this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
    this.active=n;
  }
  add(index,color){
    const s=++this.samples[index];
    const o=index*3;
    this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
    const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
    const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
    this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
    this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
  }
  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

class ProgressiveAccumulator{
  constructor(width,height){
    this.width=width;this.height=height;
    const n=width*height;
    this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
    this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
    this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
    this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
    this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
    this.active=n;
  }
  add(index,color){
    const s=++this.samples[index];
    const o=index*3;
    this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
    const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
    const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
    this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
    this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
  }
  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

const RenderGroup8Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'multi-bounce path integration',
    'path throughput',
    'secondary-ray spawning',
    'cosine hemisphere BSDF sampling baseline',
    'environment contribution on path miss',
    'path-GI render mode'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B ray/BVH tracing',
    'Core C physical materials and lights',
    'Group 7 shadow visibility and direct-light sampling'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'progressive accumulation',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'MIS',
    'Light Cache',
    'denoising',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  PathSampler,PathBSDFSampler,PathIntegrator,PathGIRenderer,RenderGroup8Contract
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

function runRenderGroup10SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const F=DielectricTransport.fresnelDielectric(1,1,1.5);
    expect(Number.isFinite(F)&&F>0&&F<.1,'Normal-incidence dielectric Fresnel failed');
    expect(DielectricTransport.fresnelDielectric(.01,1.5,1)>.5,'High-angle internal Fresnel failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Fresnel test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const r=DielectricTransport.refract([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1/1.5);
    expect(r&&r.every(Number.isFinite)&&r[2]<-.99,'Normal-incidence refraction failed');
    const tir=DielectricTransport.refract([.99,0,.141067],[0,0,-1],1.5);
    expect(tir===null||tir.every(Number.isFinite),'TIR handling produced invalid direction');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Refraction test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=DielectricTransport.beerLambert([.25,.5,1],2,2);
    expect(a.every(Number.isFinite),'Absorption produced non-finite values');
    expect(Math.abs(a[0]-.25)<1e-6&&Math.abs(a[2]-1)<1e-6,'Absorption distance scaling failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Absorption test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const m=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'glass',name:'Glass',shading:'physical',baseColor:[1,1,1],
      roughness:.1,metalness:0,opacity:1,transparent:true,
      transmission:1,ior:1.5,thickness:2,attenuationColor:[.8,.9,1],attenuationDistance:5,
      emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    expect(m.transmission===1&&Math.abs(m.ior-1.5)<1e-9&&m.thickness===2,'Transmission material fields failed');
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(m,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],9,0);
    expect(s&&s.transmission===true&&s.direction.every(Number.isFinite),'Transmission BSDF sample failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Transmission material test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'emit',name:'Emit',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],roughness:.5,metalness:0,
      opacity:1,transmission:0,ior:1.5,thickness:0,attenuationColor:[1,1,1],
      emissive:[1,.5,.25],emissiveIntensity:10
    });
    const lib={get:id=>id==='emit'?mat:null};
    const scene={objects:[{materialIds:['emit']}]};
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'o',positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array(6),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);store.finalize();
    const acc={store};
    const set=EmissiveTriangleSet.compile({renderScene:scene},acc,{materials:lib});
    expect(set.triangles.length===1&&set.totalArea>0,'Emissive triangle extraction failed');
    const sample=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(set,7,0,[0,0,2]);
    expect(sample&&sample.pdf>0&&sample.radiance.some(v=>v>0),'Emissive triangle sampling failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Emissive GI test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup10(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof DielectricTransport?.refract!=='function')errors.push('Dielectric refraction missing');
  if(typeof DielectricTransport?.fresnelDielectric!=='function')errors.push('Dielectric Fresnel missing');
  if(typeof EmissiveTriangleSet?.compile!=='function')errors.push('Emissive triangle compiler missing');
  if(typeof EmissiveTriangleSet?.sample!=='function')errors.push('Emissive triangle sampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler?.sample!=='function')errors.push('Path BSDF sampler missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup10SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function runRenderGroup9SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const w=RenderMIS.power(.5,.5);
    expect(Math.abs(w-.5)<1e-9,'MIS equal-PDF power heuristic failed');
    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=new ProgressiveAccumulator(2,1);
    a.add(0,[1,1,1]);a.add(0,[1,1,1]);
    a.add(1,[0,0,0]);a.add(1,[2,2,2]);
    expect(a.samples[0]===2&&a.samples[1]===2,'Accumulator sample count failed');
    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      progressive:true,adaptive:true,minSamples:4,maxSamples:32,noiseThreshold:.02
    });
    expect(s.progressive&&s.adaptive,'Sampling toggles lost');
    expect(s.minSamples===4&&s.maxSamples===32&&Math.abs(s.noiseThreshold-.02)<1e-9,'Sampling settings lost');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Sampling settings test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fake={type:'rectangle',width:2,height:2};
    const pdf=Group9DirectMIS.lightPdf(fake,{area:4,distance:2,emitterCos:1});
    expect(Math.abs(pdf-1)<1e-9,'Area light solid-angle PDF conversion failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Direct MIS PDF test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function runRenderGroup9SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const w=RenderMIS.power(.5,.5);
    expect(Math.abs(w-.5)<1e-9,'MIS equal-PDF power heuristic failed');
    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=new ProgressiveAccumulator(2,1);
    a.add(0,[1,1,1]);a.add(0,[1,1,1]);
    a.add(1,[0,0,0]);a.add(1,[2,2,2]);
    expect(a.samples[0]===2&&a.samples[1]===2,'Accumulator sample count failed');
    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      progressive:true,adaptive:true,minSamples:4,maxSamples:32,noiseThreshold:.02
    });
    expect(s.progressive&&s.adaptive,'Sampling toggles lost');
    expect(s.minSamples===4&&s.maxSamples===32&&Math.abs(s.noiseThreshold-.02)<1e-9,'Sampling settings lost');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Sampling settings test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fake={type:'rectangle',width:2,height:2};
    const pdf=Group9DirectMIS.lightPdf(fake,{area:4,distance:2,emitterCos:1});
    expect(Math.abs(pdf-1)<1e-9,'Area light solid-angle PDF conversion failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Direct MIS PDF test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup9(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderMIS?.power!=='function')errors.push('MIS helper missing');
  if(typeof GGXImportanceSampler?.sampleHalfVector!=='function')errors.push('GGX importance sampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler?.pdf!=='function')errors.push('BSDF PDF missing');
  if(typeof Group9DirectMIS?.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('Direct MIS evaluator missing');
  if(typeof ProgressiveAccumulator!=='function')errors.push('Progressive accumulator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer?.render!=='function')errors.push('Progressive Path renderer missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup9SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderGroup9Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.22-group9-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup9,
  mis:RenderMIS,
  ggx:GGXImportanceSampler,
  directMIS:Group9DirectMIS
});
Object.assign(window,{
  RenderMIS,GGXImportanceSampler,Group9DirectMIS,ProgressiveAccumulator,
  RenderGroup9Contract,runRenderGroup9SelfTests,validateRenderGroup9,RenderGroup9Core

const RenderGroup10Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'dielectric Fresnel transport',
    'refraction and total-internal-reflection handling',
    'transmission ray classification',
    'basic absorption/attenuation transport',
    'emissive triangle extraction',
    'emissive triangle next-event sampling'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Group 9 path sampling, MIS and progressive accumulation',
    'Core B triangle store and acceleration',
    'Core C material compilation',
    'Part 4 shadow visibility'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'nested dielectric medium stacks',

    'caustic-specialized sampling',
    'Light Cache',
    'denoising',

    'path guiding'
  ])

function runRenderGroup10SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const F=DielectricTransport.fresnelDielectric(1,1,1.5);
    expect(Number.isFinite(F)&&F>0&&F<.1,'Normal-incidence dielectric Fresnel failed');
    expect(DielectricTransport.fresnelDielectric(.01,1.5,1)>.5,'High-angle internal Fresnel failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Fresnel test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const r=DielectricTransport.refract([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1/1.5);
    expect(r&&r.every(Number.isFinite)&&r[2]<-.99,'Normal-incidence refraction failed');
    const tir=DielectricTransport.refract([.99,0,.141067],[0,0,-1],1.5);
    expect(tir===null||tir.every(Number.isFinite),'TIR handling produced invalid direction');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Refraction test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=DielectricTransport.beerLambert([.25,.5,1],2,2);
    expect(a.every(Number.isFinite),'Absorption produced non-finite values');
    expect(Math.abs(a[0]-.25)<1e-6&&Math.abs(a[2]-1)<1e-6,'Absorption distance scaling failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Absorption test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const m=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'glass',name:'Glass',shading:'physical',baseColor:[1,1,1],
      roughness:.1,metalness:0,opacity:1,transparent:true,
      transmission:1,ior:1.5,thickness:2,attenuationColor:[.8,.9,1],attenuationDistance:5,
      emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    expect(m.transmission===1&&Math.abs(m.ior-1.5)<1e-9&&m.thickness===2,'Transmission material fields failed');
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(m,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],9,0);
    expect(s&&s.transmission===true&&s.direction.every(Number.isFinite),'Transmission BSDF sample failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Transmission material test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'emit',name:'Emit',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],roughness:.5,metalness:0,
      opacity:1,transmission:0,ior:1.5,thickness:0,attenuationColor:[1,1,1],
      emissive:[1,.5,.25],emissiveIntensity:10
    });
    const lib={get:id=>id==='emit'?mat:null};
    const scene={objects:[{materialIds:['emit']}]};
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'o',positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array(6),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);store.finalize();
    const acc={store};
    const set=EmissiveTriangleSet.compile({renderScene:scene},acc,{materials:lib});
    expect(set.triangles.length===1&&set.totalArea>0,'Emissive triangle extraction failed');
    const sample=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(set,7,0,[0,0,2]);
    expect(sample&&sample.pdf>0&&sample.radiance.some(v=>v>0),'Emissive triangle sampling failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Emissive GI test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup10(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof DielectricTransport?.refract!=='function')errors.push('Dielectric refraction missing');
  if(typeof DielectricTransport?.fresnelDielectric!=='function')errors.push('Dielectric Fresnel missing');
  if(typeof EmissiveTriangleSet?.compile!=='function')errors.push('Emissive triangle compiler missing');
  if(typeof EmissiveTriangleSet?.sample!=='function')errors.push('Emissive triangle sampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler?.sample!=='function')errors.push('Path BSDF sampler missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup10SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
