    'emissive triangle GI preparation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B acceleration',
    'Core C material/light compilation',
    'Core D path/direct-light services',
    'Part 6 framebuffer/settings progress'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor geometry or selection',
    'Light Cache',
    'AOV architecture',
    'denoising',
    'Light Tree',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderCoreESelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      engine:'path',progressive:true,adaptive:true,minSamples:4,maxSamples:32,
      noiseThreshold:.02,maxBounces:6
    });
    expect(s.progressive&&s.adaptive,'Core E lost Group 9 sampling settings');
    expect(s.minSamples===4&&s.maxSamples===32,'Core E sample range mismatch');
    expect(s.maxBounces===6,'Core E bounce policy mismatch');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Core E settings: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const glass=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'ce-glass',name:'Core E Glass',shading:'physical',baseColor:[1,1,1],
      roughness:.15,metalness:0,opacity:1,transparent:true,transmission:1,ior:1.5,
      thickness:1,attenuationColor:[.9,.95,1],attenuationDistance:4,
      emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const sample=PathBSDFSampler.sample(glass,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],1337,0);
    expect(sample&&sample.direction.every(Number.isFinite),'Core E transmissive BSDF failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Core E transport: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const r=RenderCoreE.rendererFor({settings:{engine:'path',globalIllumination:true}});
    expect(r===PathGIRenderer,'Core E Path renderer routing failed');
    con
--- occurrence 12 at 486228 ---
• ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
    }else if(region){
      region=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!region)return false;
    }
    if(RendererRuntimeState.degraded){
      const criticalReady=
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.createRequest==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.prepare==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.rendererFor==='function' &&
        !!this.jobs &&
        !!this.ui?.frameBuffer;

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        RendererBootstrap.showFailure(job.error);
        return job;
      }
    }
    try{
      const snapshotStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
      job=RendererMasterCore.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'snapshot',snapshotStart16);
      this.jobs?.configureThreads?.(job.settings.cpuThreads);
    }catch(err){
      job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'snapshot',job.error);
      this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice)notice.innerHTML='Render snapshot failed.<br>'+String(err?.message||err);
      console.error('3DLite Render snapshot:',err);
      return job;
    }
    this.lastSettings=job.settings;
    this.lastRegion=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.resize(...job.progress.resolution);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice)notice.innerHTML=`Render snapshot ready.<br>${job.renderScene.objects.length} object(s) • ${job.renderScene.materials.length} material(s).`;
    this.jobs.start(job);
    return job;
  },

  renderLast(){
    const settings=this.lastCompletedSettings||this.lastSettings;
    const region=this.lastCompletedSettings?this.lastCompletedRegion:this.lastRegion;
    return settings?this.render('last',region,settings):this.render();
  },

  mapDisplayedRegion(localRegion){
    return RenderRegion14.mapNested(this.lastCompletedRegion,localRegion);
  },

  renderRegion(region=null){
    if(region){
      const clean=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!clean)return false;
      this.pendingRegion=clean;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
      return this.render('region',clean);
    }

    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    if(this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame){
      this.regionSelectArmed=true;
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        notice.style.display='flex';
        notice.innerHTML='Render Region: drag on the render image to choose an area.';
      }
      return true;
    }

    const fallback=Object.freeze({x:.25,y:.25,width:.5,height:.5});
    this.pendingRegion=fallback;
    return this.render('region',fallback);
  },

  recordCompleted(job){
    if(!job||job.cancelled||job.error)return false;
    this.lastCompletedSettings=job.settings||null;
    this.lastCompletedRegion=RenderRegion14.sanitize(job.region);
    return true;
  },

  stop(){return this.jobs.cancel(true);},
  pause(){return this.jobs.pause();},
  resume(){return this.jobs.resume();},
  restart(){
    const settings=this.lastSettings||this.readSettings();
    return this.render('restart',this.lastRegion,settings);
  },

  showSettings(){this.ui.open(this.ui.settingsWindow);},
  showFrameBuffer(){this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();this.updateFrameActions();},

  updateFrameActions(){
    const valid=!!this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame;
    for(const id of ['rfSave','rfCopy']){
      const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.disabled=!valid;
    }
    return valid;
  },

  showChannel(name){
    const job=this.jobs?.activeJob||this.jobs?.lastJob;
    let frame=null;
    try{
      frame=RenderPresenter13.frame(job,name);
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('[3DLite Group13] Presentation failed:',err);
      frame=name==='Beauty'?job?.beautyFrame||null:null;
    }
    if(!frame)return false;
    this.ui.frameBuffer.channel=name;
    this.ui.frameBuffer.present(frame);
    this.updateFrameActions();
    return true;
  },

  clearFrame(){
    this.ui.frameBuffer.clear();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.textContent='No rendered frame.';}
    this.updateFrameActions?.();
  },

  saveFrame(){
    const fb=this.ui.frameBuffer;
    if(!fb?.hasValidFrame)return false;
    const requested=(this.lastCompletedSettings?.format||this.lastSettings?.format||this.readSettings().format||'PNG').toUpperCase();
    const mime=requested==='JPEG'?'image/jpeg':'image/png';
    const ext=requested==='JPEG'?'jpg':'png';
    const channel=String(fb.channel||document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,'_');
    const region=this.lastCompletedRegion?'_Region':'';
    const a=document.createElement('a');
    a.download=`3DLite_Render${region}_${channel}.${ext}`;
    a.href=fb.canvas.toDataURL(mime,requested==='JPEG'?.94:
--- occurrence 13 at 497041 ---
mpletedRegion?'_Region':'';
    const a=document.createElement('a');
    a.download=`3DLite_Render${region}_${channel}.${ext}`;
    a.href=fb.canvas.toDataURL(mime,requested==='JPEG'?.94:undefined);
    a.click();
    return true;
  },

  async copyFrame(){
    try{
      if(!this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame)return false;
      const blob=await new Promise(r=>this.ui.frameBuffer.canvas.toBlob(r,'image/png'));
      if(!blob||!navigator.clipboard?.write||typeof ClipboardItem==='undefined')return false;
      await navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({'image/png':blob})]);
      return true;
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('3DLite render copy failed:',err);
      return false;
    }
  }
};

const RenderSettingsPolicy=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(s){
    const x={...s};
    x.engine=x.engine==='path'?'path':'fast';
    x.quality=['Draft','Preview','High','Ultra','Custom'].includes(x.quality)?x.quality:'Preview';
    x.width=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.width)||1280)));
    x.height=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.height)||720)));
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Math.min(200,Number(x.percentage)||100));
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.maxBounces)||5));
    x.background=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(x.background||'')?x.background:'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Math.min(1e6,Number(x.environmentStrength)||0));
    x.cpuThreads=x.cpuThreads==='Auto'?'Auto':String(Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.cpuThreads)||1)));
    x.tileSize=String(x.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(!['Auto','8','16','32','64'].includes(x.tileSize))x.tileSize='Auto';
    x.acceleration='BVH';
    x.progressive=x.progressive!==false;
    x.adaptive=x.adaptive!==false;
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Math.round(Number(x.minSamples)||8)));
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Math.min(4096,Math.round(Number(x.maxSamples)||64)));
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0.0001,Math.min(1,Number(x.noiseThreshold)||.03));
    x.denoise=['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(x.denoise)?x.denoise:'Off';
    x.toneMapping=['ACES','Reinhard','Linear'].includes(x.toneMapping)?x.toneMapping:'ACES';
    x.exposure=Math.max(-10,Math.min(10,Number(x.exposure)||0));
    x.primaryGI='Brute Force';x.secondaryGI=['None','Brute Force','Light Cache'].includes(x.secondaryGI)?x.secondaryGI:'Brute Force';
    x.format=x.format==='JPEG'?'JPEG':'PNG';
    x.colorDepth='8-bit';
    x.lightTree=!!x.lightTree;
    x.pathGuiding=!!x.pathGuiding;
    return Object.freeze(x);
  }
});

function markRenderSettingsCustom(event){
  const id=event?.target?.id||'';
  if(!id||id==='rsQuality'||id==='rsPreset')return;
  const q=document.getElementById('rsQuality');
  if(q&&q.value!=='Custom')q.value='Custom';
}

function applyRenderQualityPreset(name){
  const table={
    Draft:{bounces:2,min:2,max:8,noise:.08},
    Preview:{bounces:5,min:8,max:64,noise:.03},
    High:{bounces:8,min:16,max:256,noise:.015},
    Ultra:{bounces:12,min:32,max:512,noise:.008}
  };
  const p=table[name];if(!p)return;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v;};
  set('rsBounces',p.bounces);set('rsMinSamples',p.min);set('rsMaxSamples',p.max);set('rsNoise',p.noise);
}


function setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState(){
  const deferred=['rsPrimaryGI'];
  for(const id of deferred){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=true;el.title='Deferred to a later renderer group';}
  }
  for(const id of ['rsGI','rsSecondaryGI','rsBounces','rsProgressive','rsAdaptive','rsMinSamples','rsMaxSamples','rsNoise','rsDenoise','rsToneMap','rsExposure','rsLightTree','rsPathGuiding']){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=false;el.title='';}
  }
  const denoise=document.getElementById('rsDenoise');if(denoise&&!['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(denoise.value))denoise.value='Off';

  const format=document.getElementById('rsFormat');
  if(format){
    [...format.options].forEach(o=>{if(o.value==='EXR'||o.text==='EXR')o.disabled=true;});
    if(format.value==='EXR')format.value='PNG';
  }
  const depth=document.getElementById('rsDepth');
  if(depth){
    [...depth.options].forEach(o=>o.disabled=o.text!=='8-bit');
    depth.value='8-bit';
    depth.title='16/32-bit output is deferred until the render channel/export architecture is implemented';
  }
}


const RenderGroup1Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'render menu',
    'render settings UI shell',
    'render framebuffer UI shell',
    'render request settings',
    'render job lifecycle state'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor scene objects',
    'MNMesh topology',
    'selection',
    'transforms',
    'viewport materials',
    'editor undo history',
    'render scene extraction',
    'ray tracing',
    'BVH',
    'lighting',
    'path integration'
  ]),
  extensionPoints:Object.freeze([
    'RenderFramework.createJob',
    'RenderJobController.start',
    'RenderFrameBufferView',
    'RenderFramework.render'
  ])
});
window.RenderGroup1Contract=RenderGroup1Contract;

function validateRenderGroup1(){
  const errors=[];
  const ids=[
    'renderMenuBtn','renderMenuPopup','renderSettingsWindow','renderFrameWindow',
    'renderNowBtn','renderSettingsBtn','renderLastBtn','renderRegionBtn',
    'renderStopBtn','showRenderWindowBtn','renderCanvas','renderProgress'
  ];
  for(const id of ids)if(!document.getElementById(id))errors.push('Missing '+id);
  if(!(RenderFramework.settings instanceof RenderSettingsModel))errors.push('Render settings model unavailable');
  if(!(RenderFramework.ui instanceof RenderUIShell))errors.push('Render UI shell unavailable');
  if(typeof RenderFramework.render!=='function')errors.push('Render API unavailable');
  if(!RenderGroup1Contract?.extensionPoints?.length)errors.push('Render Group 1 contract unavailable');
  if(RenderFramework.initialized && !RenderFramework.jobs)errors.push('Render job controller unavailable');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function initRenderGroup1(){
  if(RenderFramework.initialized)return true;

  RenderFramework.ui.cache();
  setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState();
  bindLightUI();
  RenderFramework.jobs=new RenderJobController(p=>RenderFramework.ui.updateProgress(p));
  const configuredThreads=RenderFramework.settings.cpuThreads;
  if(configuredThreads!=='Auto'){
    RenderFramework.jobs.workerPool.size=Math.max(1,Math.min(Number(configuredThreads)||1,16));
  }

  const menuBtn=document.getElementById('renderMenuBtn');
  const menu=RenderFramework.ui.menu;

  menuBtn?.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const r=menuBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
    menu.style.left=r.left+'px';
    menu.style.top=r.bottom+'px';
    const open=menu.classList.toggle('open');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-hidden',open?'false':'true');
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(menu?.classList.contains('open')&&!menu.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==menuBtn){
      menu.classList.remove('open');
      menu.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('renderNowBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.render());
  document.getElementById('renderSettingsBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.showSettings());
  document.getElementById('renderLastBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.renderLast());
  document.getElementById('renderRegionBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.renderRegion());
  document.getElementById('renderStopBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.stop());
  document.getElementById('showRenderWindowBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.showFrameBuffer());
  document.getElementById('renderSettingsRenderBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.render());

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-render-close="settings"]').forEach(
    b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.close(RenderFramework.ui.settingsWindow))
  );
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-render-close="frame"]').forEach(
    b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.close(RenderFramework.ui.frameWindow))
  );

  document.getElementById('rfStop')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.stop());
  document.getElementById('rfPause')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.pause());
  document.getElementById('rfResume')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.resume());
  document.getElementById('rfRestart')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.restart());
  document.getElementById('rfSave')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.saveFrame());
  document.getElementById('rfCopy')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.copyFrame());
  document.getElementById('rfFit')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.fit());
  document.getElementById('rfOne')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.setZoom(1));
  document.getElementById('rfZoomIn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.setZoom(RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.zoom*1.25));
  document.getElementById('rfZoomOut')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.setZoom(RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.zoom/1.25));
  document.getElementById('rfRegion')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.renderRegion());

  {
    const canvas=document.getElementById('renderCanvas');
    let drag=null;
    let overlay=null;

    const clearOverlay=()=>{
      if(overlay)overlay.remove();
      overlay=null;
    };

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(!RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed)return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(rect.width,e.clientX-rect.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(rect.height,e.clientY-rect.top));
      drag={x,y,rect,pointerId:e.pointerId};

      clearOverlay();
      overlay=document.createElement('div');
      overlay.style.cssText='position:fixed;border:1px dashed #fff;pointer-events:none;z-index:100000;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #000;';
      document.body.appendChild(overlay);
      canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
    });

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||!overlay)return;
      const r=drag.rect;
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.width,e.clientX-r.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.height,e.clientY-r.top));
      const left=Math.min(drag.x,x);
      const top=Math.min(drag.y,y);
      const width=Math.abs(x-drag.x);
      const height=Math.abs(y-drag.y);

      overlay.style.left=(r.left+left)+'px';
      overlay.style.top=(r.top+top)+'px';
      overlay.style.width=width+'px';
      overlay.style.height=height+'px';
    });

    const finishRegion=e=>{
      if(!drag)return;
      const r=drag.rect;
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.width,e.clientX-r.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.height,e.clientY-r.top));

  }

  dispose(){
    if(this.disposed)return;
    this.disposed=true;
    this.generation++;

    for(const task of this.queue){
      task.reject(new WorkerError('Worker pool disposed'));
    }
    this.queue=[];

    for(const task of this.activeTasks.values()){
      task.reject(new WorkerError('Worker pool disposed'));
    }
    this.activeTasks.clear();

    for(const worker of [...this.workers]){
      try{worker.terminate();}catch(_){}
    }
    this.workers=[];this.idle=[];

    if(this.workerURL){
      URL.revokeObjectURL(this.workerURL);
      this.workerURL=null;
    }
  }
}

class PreparedGeometrySet{
  constructor(items){
    this.items=Object.freeze(items.slice());
    this.objectCount=items.length;
    this.vertexCount=items.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.vertexCount,0);
    this.triangleCount=items.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.triangleCount,0);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

var RenderGeometryPreparation={
  mainThread(job,packet){
    if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});

    const vertexCount=packet.positions.length/3;
    const triangleCount=packet.indices.length/3;

    for(let i=0;i<packet.indices.length;i++){
      if(packet.indices[i]>=vertexCount)throw new GeometryCompileError('Index out of range in main-thread worker fallback');
    }

    return {
      type:'geometryPrepared',
      jobId:job.id,
      objectId:packet.objectId,
      vertexCount,
      triangleCount,
      bounds:new Float32Array([
        ...packet.bounds.min,
        ...packet.bounds.max
      ]),
      triangleIndices:new Uint32Array(packet.indices)
    };
  },

  async prepare(job,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(job.renderScene);
    if(!packets.length)return new PreparedGeometrySet([]);

    const items=[];
    const total=packets.length;

    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});

      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled){
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,25));
      }

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;

      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){
          result=this.mainThread(job,packet);
        }else{
          throw err;
        }
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));

      onProgress((i+1)/total,items[items.length-1]);
    }

    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  }
};

class RenderExecutionGeometry{
  constructor(renderScene){
    this.accelerationPackets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
    this.workerPackets=this.accelerationPackets.map(p=>new RenderGeometryPacket({
      objectId:p.objectId,name:p.name,
      positions:new Float32Array(p.positions),
      normals:new Float32Array(p.normals),
      uvs:new Float32Array(p.uvs),
      indices:new Uint32Array(p.indices),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array(p.materialIndices),
      bounds:{min:[...p.bounds.min],max:[...p.bounds.max]}
    }));
    Object.freeze(this.accelerationPackets);
    Object.freeze(this.workerPackets);
  }
}

var RenderCoreB=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.16-core-b-part2',

  createExecutionGeometry(renderScene){
    return new RenderExecutionGeometry(renderScene);
  },

  buildAccelerationFromPackets(packets){
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
    store.finalize();
    return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
  },

  async prepareWorkerGeometry(job,pool,executionGeometry,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const items=[],packets=executionGeometry.workerPackets,total=packets.length;
    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,25));

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;
      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){job.progress.workerFallback=true;result=RenderGeometryPreparation.mainThread(job,packet);}
        else throw err;
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));
      onProgress((i+1)/Math.max(1,total),items[items.length-1]);
    }
    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g3=validateRenderGroup3();if(!g3.ok)errors.push(...g3.errors);
    const g4=validateRenderGroup4();if(!g4.ok)errors.push(...g4.errors);
    if(typeof this.createExecutionGeometry!=='function')errors.push('Core B execution geometry missing');
    if(typeof this.buildAccelerationFromPackets!=='function')errors.push('Core B acceleration builder missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreB=RenderCoreB;

function validateRenderGroup3(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool!=='function')errors.push('RenderWorkerPool missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryCompiler missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryValidation.validatePacket!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryValidation missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryPreparation.prepare!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryPreparation missing');
  if(typeof PreparedGeometrySet!=='function')errors.push('PreparedGeometrySet missing');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RayMath=Object.freeze({
  EPSILON:1e-6,
  add(a,b){return [a[0]+b[0],a[1]+b[1],a[2]+b[2]]},
  sub(a,b){return [a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1],a[2]-b[2]]},
  mul(a,s){return [a[0]*s,a[1]*s,a[2]*s]},
  dot(a,b){return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]},
  cross(a,b){return [a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]]},
  length(a){return Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])},
  normalize(a){const l=Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])||1;return [a[0]/l,a[1]/l,a[2]/l]},
  min3(a,b){return [Math.min(a[0],b[0]),Math.min(a[1],b[1]),Math.min(a[2],b[2])]},
  max3(a,b){return [Math.max(a[0],b[0]),Math.max(a[1],b[1]),Math.max(a[2],b[2])]}
});

const RenderRayType=Object.freeze({CAMERA:'camera',SHADOW:'shadow',DIFFUSE:'diffuse',GLOSSY:'glossy',TRANSMISSION:'transmission'});
class RenderRay{
  constructor(origin,direction,tMin=1e-5,tMax=Infinity,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA,depth=0){
    this.origin=origin;this.direction=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    this.invDirection=[
      Math.abs(this.direction[0])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[0],
      Math.abs(this.direction[1])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[1],
      Math.abs(this.direction[2])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[2]
    ];
    this.tMin=tMin;this.tMax=tMax;this.type=type;this.depth=depth;
  }
  at(t){return RayMath.add(this.origin,RayMath.mul(this.direction,t))}
}
const RenderRayPolicy=Object.freeze({
  epsilonForPoint(p){const scale=Math.max(1,Math.abs(p[0]),Math.abs(p[1]),Math.abs(p[2]));return Math.min(1e-2,Math.max(1e-6,scale*1e-7));}
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderRayType,RenderRayPolicy});

class RenderHit{
  constructor(){
    this.hit=false;this.t=Infinity;this.u=0;this.v=0;
    this.objectIndex=-1;this.triangleIndex=-1;
    this.position=null;this.geometricNormal=null;this.orientedGeometricNormal=null;this.frontFace=true;this.materialIndex=0;
  }
}

const TriangleIntersector=Object.freeze({
  intersect(ray,p0,p1,p2,hit=new RenderHit()){
    const e1=RayMath.sub(p1,p0),e2=RayMath.sub(p2,p0);
    const pvec=RayMath.cross(ray.direction,e2);
    const det=RayMath.dot(e1,pvec);
    if(Math.abs(det)<RayMath.EPSILON)return hit;
    const invDet=1/det;
    const tvec=RayMath.sub(ray.origin,p0);
    const u=RayMath.dot(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
    if(u<0||u>1)return hit;
    const qvec=RayMath.cross(tvec,e1);
    const v=RayMath.dot(ray.direction,qvec)*invDet;
    if(v<0||u+v>1)return hit;
    const t=RayMath.dot(e2,qvec)*invDet;
    if(t<ray.tMin||t>ray.tMax||t>=hit.t)return hit;
    hit.hit=true;hit.t=t;hit.u=u;hit.v=v;
    hit.position=ray.at(t);
    hit.geometricNormal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(e1,e2));
    hit.frontFace=RayMath.dot(ray.direction,hit.geometricNormal)<0;
    hit.orientedGeometricNormal=hit.frontFace?hit.geometricNormal:RayMath.mul(hit.geometricNormal,-1);
    return hit;
  }
});

  entry(ray,min,max,tLimit=Infinity){

const AABBIntersector=Object.freeze({
  entry(ray,min,max,tLimit=Infinity){
    let t0=ray.tMin,t1=Math.min(ray.tMax,tLimit);
    for(let axis=0;axis<3;axis++){
      const d=ray.direction[axis];
      if(Math.abs(d)<RayMath.EPSILON){
        if(ray.origin[axis]<min[axis]||ray.origin[axis]>max[axis])return Infinity;
        continue;
      }
      let a=(min[axis]-ray.origin[axis])*ray.invDirection[axis];
      let b=(max[axis]-ray.origin[axis])*ray.invDirection[axis];
      if(a>b){const tmp=a;a=b;b=tmp}
      t0=Math.max(t0,a);t1=Math.min(t1,b);
      if(t1<t0)return Infinity;
    }
    return t0;
  },
  intersect(ray,min,max,tLimit=Infinity){return Number.isFinite(this.entry(ray,min,max,tLimit))}
});

var RenderTriangleStore = class RenderTriangleStore{
  constructor(){
    this.positions=[];
    this.normals=[];
    this.uvs=[];
    this.triangles=[];
    this.materialIndices=[];
    this.objectIndices=[];
    this.objectIds=[];
    this.stats={inputTriangles:0,acceptedTriangles:0,rejectedInvalidIndex:0,rejectedNonFinite:0,rejectedDegenerate:0};
  }
  addPacket(packet,objectIndex){
    const baseVertex=this.positions.length/3;
    for(let i=0;i<packet.positions.length;i++)this.positions.push(packet.positions[i]);

    // Startup-safe cross-generation compatibility.
    // Geometry packets created by earlier diagnostic/self-test paths may not
    // contain shading normals or UVs. Missing optional attributes must not
    // abort editor initialization.
    const packetVertexCount=packet.positions.length/3;
    const srcNormals=(packet.normals&&packet.normals.length===packet.positions.length)
      ? packet.normals
      : new Float32Array(packet.positions.length);
    const srcUVs=(packet.uvs&&packet.uvs.length===packetVertexCount*2)
      ? packet.uvs
      : new Float32Array(packetVertexCount*2);

    for(let i=0;i<srcNormals.length;i++)this.normals.push(srcNormals[i]);
    for(let i=0;i<srcUVs.length;i++)this.uvs.push(srcUVs[i]);
    const idx=packet.indices;
    for(let t=0;t<idx.length;t+=3){
      this.stats.inputTriangles++;
      const ia=idx[t],ib=idx[t+1],ic=idx[t+2];
      if(!Number.isInteger(ia)||!Number.isInteger(ib)||!Number.isInteger(ic)||ia<0||ib<0||ic<0||ia>=packetVertexCount||ib>=packetVertexCount||ic>=packetVertexCount){this.stats.rejectedInvalidIndex++;continue;}

/*
  BLAS/TLAS boundary:
  - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
  - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
    so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
  Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
*/
const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
  mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
  cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
  build(scene,builder){
    const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
    if(cached)return cached;
    return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
  }
});
window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

const DiagnosticRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const cameraSnapshot=job.renderScene.camera;
    const rowChunk=8;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++){
      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

function runRenderGroup4SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],approx=(a,b,e=1e-5)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;
  try{
    let ray=new RenderRay([0,0,1],[0,0,-1]);
    let hit=TriangleIntersector.intersect(ray,[-1,-1,0],[1,-1,0],[0,1,0]);
    if(!hit.hit||!approx(hit.t,1)||!hit.frontFace||!hit.orientedGeometricNormal)errors.push('Triangle front-face hit failed');

    ray=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1]);
    hit=TriangleIntersector.intersect(ray,[-1,-1,0],[1,-1,0],[0,1,0]);
    if(!hit.hit||hit.frontFace)errors.push('Triangle back-face classification failed');

    ray=new RenderRay([3,3,1],[0,0,-1]);
    if(TriangleIntersector.intersect(ray,[-1,-1,0],[1,-1,0],[0,1,0]).hit)errors.push('Triangle miss failed');

    if(!AABBIntersector.intersect(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1]),[-1,-1,-1],[1,1,1]))errors.push('AABB hit failed');

    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({objectId:'a',positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0,  -1,-1,-3,1,-1,-3,0,1,-3]),normals:new Float32Array(18),uvs:new Float32Array(12),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2,3,4,5]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0,0])},0);
    store.finalize();
    const bvh=new RenderBVH(store,1),closest=bvh.intersect(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1]));
    if(!closest.hit||!approx(closest.t,2))errors.push('BVH closest-hit failed');
    if(!bvh.anyHit(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1],1e-5,2.1,RenderRayType.SHADOW)))errors.push('BVH any-hit failed');
    if(bvh.anyHit(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1],1e-5,1.9,RenderRayType.SHADOW)))errors.push('Finite shadow tMax failed');
    const bs=bvh.stats();if(bs.nodeCount<1||bs.leafCount<1||bs.triangleCount!==2)errors.push('BVH statistics failed');

    const legacy=new RenderTriangleStore();
    legacy.addPacket({objectId:'legacy',positions:new Float32Array([0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1,0]),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])},0);legacy.finalize();
    if(legacy.normals.length!==9||legacy.uvs.length!==6)errors.push('Legacy minimal packet compatibility failed');

    const deg=new RenderTriangleStore();
    deg.addPacket({objectId:'deg',positions:new Float32Array([0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0]),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])},0);deg.finalize();
    if(deg.triangleCount()!==0||deg.stats.rejectedDegenerate!==1)errors.push('Degenerate rejection failed');

    const empty=new RenderTriangleStore();empty.finalize();const ebvh=new RenderBVH(empty,4);
    if(ebvh.root!==-1||ebvh.intersect(new RenderRay([0,0,0],[0,0,1])).hit||ebvh.anyHit(new RenderRay([0,0,0],[0,0,1])))errors.push('Empty BVH failed');

    const c={position:[0,0,0],quaternion:[0,0,0,1],fov:60,near:0,far:100};
    const cr=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(c,49.5,49.5,100,100);
    if(cr.direction[2]>-.99||cr.type!==RenderRayType.CAMERA)errors.push('Camera center ray failed');
    const extreme=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray({position:[0,0,0],quaternion:[NaN,0,0,1],fov:1000,near:-1,far:0},0,0,1,1);
    if(!extreme.direction.every(Number.isFinite)||extreme.tMin<=0)errors.push('Camera sanitization failed');

    const small=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0]),large=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([1e6,0,0]);
    if(!(small>0&&large>=small&&large<=1e-2))errors.push('Scale-aware epsilon failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Group4 self-test exception: '+String(err?.message||err));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup4(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderRay!=='function')errors.push('RenderRay missing');
  if(typeof TriangleIntersector.intersect!=='function')errors.push('Triangle intersector missing');
  if(typeof RenderBVH!=='function')errors.push('RenderBVH missing');
  if(typeof RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray!=='function')errors.push('Camera ray generator missing');
  if(typeof RenderAccelerationBuilder.build!=='function')errors.push('Acceleration builder missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup4SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderColor=Object.freeze({
  clamp01(x){return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x))},
  add(a,b){return [a[0]+b[0],a[1]+b[1],a[2]+b[2]]},
  mul(a,b){
    if(Array.isArray(b))return [a[0]*b[0],a[1]*b[1],a[2]*b[2]];
    return [a[0]*b,a[1]*b,a[2]*b];
  },
  mix(a,b,t){return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t,a[2]+(b[2]-a[2])*t]},
  srgbToLinear1(x){
    x=this.clamp01(x);return x<=.04045?x/12.92:Math.pow((x+.055)/1.055,2.4);
  },
  linearToSrgb1(x){
    x=Math.max(0,x);return x<=.0031308?12.92*x:1.055*Math.pow(x,1/2.4)-.055;
  },
  srgbToLinear(c){return [this.srgbToLinear1(c[0]),this.srgbToLinear1(c[1]),this.srgbToLinear1(c[2])]},
  linearToSrgb(c){return [this.linearToSrgb1(c[0]),this.linearToSrgb1(c[1]),this.linearToSrgb1(c[2])]},
  hexToLinear(hex){
    const h=String(hex||'#202020').replace('#','');
    const n=parseInt(h.length===3?h.split('').map(x=>x+x).join(''):h,16);
    if(!Number.isFinite(n))return [0.0144,0.0144,0.0144];
    return this.srgbToLinear([((n>>16)&255)/255,((n>>8)&255)/255,(n&255)/255]);
  },
  aces(c){
    const f=x=>{
      const a=2.51,b=.03,d=2.43,e=.59,g=.14;
      return this.clamp01((x*(a*x+b))/(x*(d*x+e)+g));
    };
    return [f(c[0]),f(c[1]),f(c[2])];
  }
});

const RenderMaterialPolicy=Object.freeze({
  finiteColor(c,fallback=[.54,.54,.54]){
    if(!Array.isArray(c)||c.length<3)return fallback.slice();
    return c.slice(0,3).map((v,i)=>{
      v=Number(v);
      return Number.isFinite(v)?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v)):fallback[i];
    });
  },
  scalar(v,fallback,min,max){
    v=Number(v);
    if(!Number.isFinite(v))v=fallback;
    return Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));
  },
  shading(v){
    return ['physical','lambert','unlit'].includes(v)?v:'physical';
  }
});

var RenderRayBatch16 = class RenderRayBatch16{
  constructor(capacity=128){
    this.capacity=Math.max(1,capacity|0);
    this.origins=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.directions=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.pixelIndices=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.seeds=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.count=0;
  }
  clear(){this.count=0;return this;}
  push(ray,pixelIndex,seed){
    if(this.count>=this.capacity)return false;
    const i=this.count++,o=i*3;
    this.origins[o]=ray.origin[0];this.origins[o+1]=ray.origin[1];this.origins[o+2]=ray.origin[2];
    this.directions[o]=ray.direction[0];this.directions[o+1]=ray.direction[1];this.directions[o+2]=ray.direction[2];
    this.pixelIndices[i]=pixelIndex>>>0;this.seeds[i]=seed>>>0;
    return true;
  }
  ray(i,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const o=i*3;
    return new RenderRay(
      [this.origins[o],this.origins[o+1],this.origins[o+2]],
      [this.directions[o],this.directions[o+1],this.directions[o+2]],
      1e-6,Infinity,type,0
    );
  }
  trace(acceleration,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const hits=new Array(this.count);
    for(let i=0;i<this.count;i++)hits[i]=acceleration.trace(this.ray(i,type));
    return hits;
  }
}
window.RenderRayBatch16=RenderRayBatch16;

var RenderWorkQueue16 = class RenderWorkQueue16{
  constructor(chunks){
    this.queue=chunks.slice();
    this.completed=0;
    this.claimed=0;
    this.steals=0;
  }
  static fromIndices(indices,batchSize=128){
    const chunks=[];
    const n=indices.length;
    const adaptive=Math.max(16,Math.min(512,Math.round(batchSize*(n>250000?.5:n<10000?2:1))));
    for(let i=0;i<n;i+=adaptive){
      chunks.push(typeof indices.subarray==='function'?indices.subarray(i,Math.min(n,i+adaptive)):indices.slice(i,i+adaptive));
    }
    return new RenderWorkQueue16(chunks);
  }
  splitHeavy(maxChunk=128){
    const next=[];
    for(const chunk of this.queue){
      if(chunk.length>maxChunk){
        const mid=Math.ceil(chunk.length/2);
        next.push(chunk.slice(0,mid),chunk.slice(mid));
      }else next.push(chunk);
    }
    this.queue=next;
    return this;
  }
  claim(){
    const work=this.queue.shift()||null;
    if(work){if(this.claimed>0)this.steals++;this.claimed++;}
    return work;
  }
  async run(workerFn,concurrency=1){
    const runners=[];
    const n=Math.max(1,Math.min(8,Number(concurrency)||1));
    for(let w=0;w<n;w++)runners.push((async()=>{
      for(;;){
        const batch=this.claim();
        if(!batch)break;
        await workerFn(batch,w);
        this.completed++;
      }
    })());
    await Promise.all(runners);
    return this.completed;
  }
}
window.RenderWorkQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16;

var RenderTileScheduler16=Object.freeze({
  makeTiles(width,height,tileSize){
    const size=Math.max(4,Math.min(128,Number(tileSize)||16));
    const tiles=[];
    let id=0;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y+=size){
      for(let x=0;x<width;x+=size){
        tiles.push(Object.freeze({
          id:id++,x,y,
          width:Math.min(size,width-x),
          height:Math.min(size,height-y)
        }));
      }
    }
    return tiles;
  },
  async runLocal(job,tiles,runTile,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const total=Math.max(1,tiles.length);
    for(let i=0;i<tiles.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      await runTile(tiles[i],i);
      onProgress((i+1)/total);
      if((i&1)===1)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
  }
});
window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){

window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

var RenderPerformanceMetrics=Object.freeze({
  begin(job){job.startedAt=performance.now();job.finishedAt=0;return job.startedAt;},
  beginTracing(job){job.renderStartedAt=performance.now();},
  finish(job){
    job.finishedAt=performance.now();
    job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,job.finishedAt-(job.startedAt||job.finishedAt));
    const seconds=Math.max(1e-6,(job.finishedAt-(job.renderStartedAt||job.startedAt||job.finishedAt))/1000);
    const measured=RenderCoreH.performance(job);
    const actualRays=Math.max(0,Number(measured?.totalRays)||0);
    job.progress.raysPerSecond=actualRays?Math.round(actualRays/seconds):0;
    job.performance=Object.freeze({
      totalMs:job.progress.elapsedMs,
      traceMs:seconds*1000,
      actualRays,
      raysPerSecond:job.progress.raysPerSecond,
      samples:Number(measured?.samples)||0,
      yields:Number(measured?.yields)||0,
      bvhBuildMs:job.progress.bvhBuildMs
    });
    return job.performance;
  }
});

class RenderJobController{
  constructor(onProgress){
    this.activeJob=null;
    this.lastJob=null;
    this.onProgress=onProgress||(()=>{});
    this.workerPool=new RenderWorkerPool();
    this.generation=0;
  }

  configureThreads(setting){
    return this.workerPool.setThreadSetting(setting);
  }

  workerStats(){
    return this.workerPool.stats();
  }

  start(job){
    if(this.activeJob&&!this.activeJob.finished&&!this.activeJob.cancelled)this.cancel(false);
    this.configureThreads(job?.settings?.cpuThreads||'Auto');
    this.activeJob=job;
    this.lastJob=job;
    RenderPerformanceMetrics.begin(job);
    const generation=++this.generation;

    job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.percent=0;
    this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

    this._prepare(job,generation);
    return job;
  }

  async _prepare(job,generation){
    try{
      if(!job.renderScene)throw new Error('Render job missing RenderSceneSnapshot');

      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=this.workerPool.available;
      job.progress.threads=this.workerPool.size;
      const prepared=await RendererMasterCore.prepare(job,this.workerPool,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=Math.max(0,Math.min(95,Math.round(ratio*95)));
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });
      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
      job.executionGeometry=prepared.geometry;job.preparedGeometry=prepared.prepared;job.acceleration=prepared.acceleration;job.shadingContext=prepared.shading;job.emissiveTriangles=prepared.shading?.emissiveTriangles||null;
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.percent=96;this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`
        : 'Direct';
      const activeRenderer=RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job);
      const diagnostic=await activeRenderer.render(job,job.acceleration,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        if(job.previewFrame){
          try{RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(job.previewFrame);RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();}catch(_){}
        }
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.beautyFrame=diagnostic;
      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

      Math.max(1,Math.round(sett

    if(typeof RenderCore1.createRequest!=='function')errors.push('Core 1 request API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCore1.createExecution!=='function')errors.push('Core 1 execution API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCore2.compileShading!=='function')errors.push('Core 2 shading API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCoreE.prepareShading!=='function'||typeof RenderCoreE.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Core E API missing');
    if(typeof this.prepare!=='function'||typeof this.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Master Core orchestration API missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderCore1,RenderCore2,RenderCoreE,RendererMasterCore});
var RenderPerformanceMetrics=Object.freeze({
  begin(job){job.startedAt=performance.now();job.finishedAt=0;return job.startedAt;},
  beginTracing(job){job.renderStartedAt=performance.now();},
  finish(job){
    job.finishedAt=performance.now();
    job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,job.finishedAt-(job.startedAt||job.finishedAt));
    const seconds=Math.max(1e-6,(job.finishedAt-(job.renderStartedAt||job.startedAt||job.finishedAt))/1000);
    const measured=RenderCoreH.performance(job);
    const actualRays=Math.max(0,Number(measured?.totalRays)||0);
    job.progress.raysPerSecond=actualRays?Math.round(actualRays/seconds):0;
    job.performance=Object.freeze({
      totalMs:job.progress.elapsedMs,
      traceMs:seconds*1000,
      actualRays,
      raysPerSecond:job.progress.raysPerSecond,
      samples:Number(measured?.samples)||0,
      yields:Number(measured?.yields)||0,
      bvhBuildMs:job.progress.bvhBuildMs
    });
    return job.performance;
  }
});

class RenderJobController{
  constructor(onProgress){
    this.activeJob=null;
    this.lastJob=null;
    this.onProgress=onProgress||(()=>{});
    this.workerPool=new RenderWorkerPool();
    this.generation=0;
  }

  configureThreads(setting){
    return this.workerPool.setThreadSetting(setting);
  }

  workerStats(){
    return this.workerPool.stats();
  }

  start(job){
    if(this.activeJob&&!this.activeJob.finished&&!this.activeJob.cancelled)this.cancel(false);
    this.configureThreads(job?.settings?.cpuThreads||'Auto');
    this.activeJob=job;
    this.lastJob=job;
    RenderPerformanceMetrics.begin(job);
    const generation=++this.generation;

    job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.percent=0;
    this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

    this._prepare(job,generation);
    return job;
  }

  async _prepare(job,generation){
    try{
      if(!job.renderScene)throw new Error('Render job missing RenderSceneSnapshot');

      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=this.workerPool.available;
      job.progress.threads=this.workerPool.size;
      const prepared=await RendererMasterCore.prepare(job,this.workerPool,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=Math.max(0,Math.min(95,Math.round(ratio*95)));
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });
      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
      job.executionGeometry=prepared.geometry;job.preparedGeometry=prepared.prepared;job.acceleration=prepared.acceleration;job.shadingContext=prepared.shading;job.emissiveTriangles=prepared.shading?.emissiveTriangles||null;
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.percent=96;this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`
        : 'Direct';
      const activeRenderer=RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job);
      const diagnostic=await activeRenderer.render(job,job.acceleration,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        if(job.previewFrame){
          try{RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(job.previewFrame);RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();}catch(_){}
        }
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.beautyFrame=diagnostic;
      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;

    if(typeof RenderCore2.compileShading!=='function')errors.push('Core 2 shading API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCoreE.prepareShading!=='function'||typeof RenderCoreE.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Core E API missing');
    if(typeof this.prepare!=='function'||typeof this.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Master Core orchestration API missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});

    this.lastJob=job;

    job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.percent=0;
    this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

    this._prepare(job,generation);
    return job;
