      if(!job.renderScene)throw new Error('Render job missing RenderSceneSnapshot');

      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=this.workerPool.available;
      job.progress.threads=this.workerPool.size;
      const prepared=await RendererMasterCore.prepare(job,this.workerPool,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=Math.max(0,Math.min(95,Math.round(ratio*95)));
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });
      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
      job.executionGeometry=prepared.geometry;job.preparedGeometry=prepared.prepared;job.acceleration=prepared.acceleration;job.shadingContext=prepared.shading;job.emissiveTriangles=prepared.shading?.emissiveTriangles||null;
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.percent=96;this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`
        : 'Direct';
      const activeRenderer=RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job);
      const diagnostic=await activeRenderer.render(job,job.acceleration,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        if(job.previewFrame){
          try{RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(job.previewFrame);RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();}catch(_){}
        }
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.beautyFrame=diagnostic;
      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
    }else if(region){
      region=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!region)return false;
    }
    if(RendererRuntimeState.degraded){
      const criticalReady=
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.createRequest==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.prepare==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.rendererFor==='function' &&
        !!this.jobs &&
        !!this.ui?.frameBuffer;

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        RendererBootstrap.showFailure(job.error);
        return job;
      }
    }
    try{
      const snapshotStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
      job=RendererMasterCore.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'snapshot',snapshotStart16);
      this.jobs?.configureThreads?.(job.settings.cpuThreads);
    }catch(err){
      job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      job.erro
--- occurrence 10 at 482778 ---
turn this.workerPool.stats();
  }

  start(job){
    if(this.activeJob&&!this.activeJob.finished&&!this.activeJob.cancelled)this.cancel(false);
    this.configureThreads(job?.settings?.cpuThreads||'Auto');
    this.activeJob=job;
    this.lastJob=job;
    RenderPerformanceMetrics.begin(job);
    const generation=++this.generation;

    job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.percent=0;
    this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

    this._prepare(job,generation);
    return job;
  }

  async _prepare(job,generation){
    try{
      if(!job.renderScene)throw new Error('Render job missing RenderSceneSnapshot');

      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=this.workerPool.available;
      job.progress.threads=this.workerPool.size;
      const prepared=await RendererMasterCore.prepare(job,this.workerPool,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=Math.max(0,Math.min(95,Math.round(ratio*95)));
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });
      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
      job.executionGeometry=prepared.geometry;job.preparedGeometry=prepared.prepared;job.acceleration=prepared.acceleration;job.shadingContext=prepared.shading;job.emissiveTriangles=prepared.shading?.emissiveTriangles||null;
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.percent=96;this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`
        : 'Direct';
      const activeRenderer=RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job);
      const diagnostic=await activeRenderer.render(job,job.acceleration,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        if(job.previewFrame){
          try{RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(job.previewFrame);RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();}catch(_){}
        }
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.beautyFrame=diagnostic;
      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
    }else if(region){
      region=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!region)return false;
    }
    if(RendererRuntimeState.degraded){
      const criticalReady=
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.createRequest==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.prepare==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.rendererFor==='function' &&
        !!this.jobs &&
        !!this.ui?.frameBuffer;

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        RendererBootstrap.showFailure(job.error);
        return job;
      }
    }
    try{
      const snapshotStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
      job=RendererMasterCore.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'snapshot',snapshotStart16);
      this.jobs?.configureThreads?.(job.settings.cpuThreads);
    }catch(err){
      job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'snapshot',job.error);
      this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice)notice.innerHTML='Render snapshot failed.<br>'+String(err?.message||err);
      console.error('3DLite Render snapshot:',err);
      return job;
    }
    this.lastSettings=job.settings;
    this.lastRegion=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.resize(...job.progress.resolution);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice)notice.innerHTML=`Render snapshot ready.<br>${job.renderScene.objects.length} object(s) • ${job.renderScene.materials.length} material(s).`;
    this.jobs.start(job);
    return job;
  },

  renderLast(){
    const settings=this.lastCompletedSettings||this.lastSettings;
    const region=this.lastCompletedSettings?this.lastCompletedRegion:this.lastRegion;
    return settings?this.render('last',region,settings):this.render();
  },

  mapDisplayedRegion(localRegion){
    return RenderRegion14.mapNested(this.lastCompletedRegion,localRegion);
  },

  renderRegion(region=null){
    if(region){
      const clean=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!clean)return false;
      this.pendingRegion=clean;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
      return this.render('region',clean);
    }

    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    if(this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame){
      this.regionSelectArmed=true;
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        no
--- occurrence 12 at 484151 ---
dGeometry=prepared.prepared;job.acceleration=prepared.acceleration;job.shadingContext=prepared.shading;job.emissiveTriangles=prepared.shading?.emissiveTriangles||null;
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING;job.progress.percent=96;this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`
        : 'Direct';
      const activeRenderer=RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job);
      const diagnostic=await activeRenderer.render(job,job.acceleration,ratio=>{
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);
        job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
        if(job.previewFrame){
          try{RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(job.previewFrame);RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();}catch(_){}
        }
        this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.beautyFrame=diagnostic;
      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

function applyRenderQualityPreset(name){
  const table={
    Draft:{bounces:2,min:2,max:8,noise:.08},
    Preview:{bounces:5,min:8,max:64,noise:.03},
    High:{bounces:8,min:16,max:256,noise:.015},
    Ultra:{bounces:12,min:32,max:512,noise:.008}
  };
  const p=table[name];if(!p)return;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v;};
  set('rsBounces',p.bounces);set('rsMinSamples',p.min);set('rsMaxSamples',p.max);set('rsNoise',p.noise);
}


function setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState(){
  const deferred=['rsPrimaryGI'];
  for(const id of deferred){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=true;el.title='Deferred to a later renderer group';}
  }
  for(const id of ['rsGI','rsSecondaryGI','rsBounces','rsProgressive','rsAdaptive','rsMinSamples','rsMaxSamples','rsNoise','rsDenoise','rsToneMap','rsExposure','rsLightTree','rsPathGuiding']){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=false;el.title='';}
  }
  const denoise=document.getElementById('rsDenoise');if(denoise&&!['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(denoise.value))denoise.value='Off';

  const format=document.getElementById('rsFormat');
  if(format){
    [...format.options].forEach(o=>{if(o.value==='EXR'||o.text==='EXR')o.disabled=true;});
    if(format.value==='EXR')format.value='PNG';
  }
  const depth=document.getElementById('rsDepth');
  if(depth){
    [...depth.options].forEach(o=>o.disabled=o.text!=='8-bit');
    depth.value='8-bit';
    depth.title='16/32-bit output is deferred until the render channel/export architecture is implemented';
  }
}

function validateRenderGroup1(){
  const errors=[];
  const ids=[
    'renderMenuBtn','renderMenuPopup','renderSettingsWindow','renderFrameWindow',
    'renderNowBtn','renderSettingsBtn','renderLastBtn','renderRegionBtn',
    'renderStopBtn','showRenderWindowBtn','renderCanvas','renderProgress'
  ];
  for(const id of ids)if(!document.getElementById(id))errors.push('Missing '+id);
  if(!(RenderFramework.settings instanceof RenderSettingsModel))errors.push('Render settings model unavailable');
  if(!(RenderFramework.ui instanceof RenderUIShell))errors.push('Render UI shell unavailable');
  if(typeof RenderFramework.render!=='function')errors.push('Render API unavailable');
  if(!RenderGroup1Contract?.extensionPoints?.length)errors.push('Render Group 1 contract unavailable');
  if(RenderFramework.initialized && !RenderFramework.jobs)errors.push('Render job controller unavailable');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function initRenderGroup1(){
  if(RenderFramework.initialized)return true;

  RenderFramework.ui.cache();
  setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState();
  bindLightUI();
  RenderFramework.jobs=new RenderJobController(p=>RenderFramework.ui.updateProgress(p));
  const configuredThreads=RenderFramework.settings.cpuThreads;
  if(configuredThreads!=='Auto'){
    RenderFramework.jobs.workerPool.size=Math.max(1,Math.min(Number(configuredThreads)||1,16));
  }

  const menuBtn=document.getElementById('renderMenuBtn');
  const menu=RenderFramework.ui.menu;

  menuBtn?.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const r=menuBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
    menu.style.left=r.left+'px';
    menu.style.top=r.bottom+'px';
    const open=menu.classList.toggle('open');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-hidden',open?'false':'true');
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(menu?.classList.contains('open')&&!menu.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==menuBtn){
      menu.classList.remove('open');
      menu.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('renderNowBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.render());
  document.getElementById('renderSettingsBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.showSettings());
  document.getElementById('renderLastBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.renderLast());
  document.getElementById('renderRegionBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.renderRegion());
  document.getElementById('renderStopBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.stop());
  document.getElementById('showRenderWindowBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.showFrameBuffer());
  document.getElementById('renderSettingsRenderBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.render());

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-render-close="settings"]').forEach(
    b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.close(RenderFramework.ui.settingsWindow))
  );
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-render-close="frame"]').forEach(
    b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.close(RenderFramework.ui.frameWindow))
  );

  document.getElementById('rfStop')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.stop());
  document.getElementById('rfPause')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.pause());
  document.getElementById('rfResume')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.resume());
  document.getElementById('rfRestart')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.restart());
  document.getElementById('rfSave')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.saveFrame());
  document.getElementById('rfCopy')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.copyFrame());
  document.getElementById('rfFit')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.fit());
  document.getElementById('rfOne')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.setZoom(1));
  document.getElementById('rfZoomIn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.setZoom(RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.zoom*1.25));
  document.getElementById('rfZoomOut')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.setZoom(RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.zoom/1.25));
  document.getElementById('rfRegion')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.renderRegion());

  {
    const canvas=document.getElementById('renderCanvas');
    let drag=null;
    let overlay=null;

    const clearOverlay=()=>{
      if(overlay)overlay.remove();
      overlay=null;
    };

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(!RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed)return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(rect.width,e.clientX-rect.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(rect.height,e.clientY-rect.top));
      drag={x,y,rect,pointerId:e.pointerId};

      clearOverlay();
      overlay=document.createElement('div');
      overlay.style.cssText='position:fixed;border:1px dashed #fff;pointer-events:none;z-index:100000;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #000;';
      document.body.appendChild(overlay);
      canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
    });

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||!overlay)return;
      const r=drag.rect;
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.width,e.clientX-r.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.height,e.clientY-r.top));
      const left=Math.min(drag.x,x);
      const top=Math.min(drag.y,y);
      const width=Math.abs(x-drag.x);
      const height=Math.abs(y-drag.y);

      overlay.style.left=(r.left+left)+'px';
      overlay.style.top=(r.top+top)+'px';
      overlay.style.width=width+'px';
      overlay.style.height=height+'px';
    });

    const finishRegion=e=>{
      if(!drag)return;
      const r=drag.rect;
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.width,e.clientX-r.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.height,e.clientY-r.top));
      const left=Math.min(drag.x,x);
      const top=Math.min(drag.y,y);
      const width=Math.abs(x-drag.x);
      const height=Math.abs(y-drag.y);

      drag=null;
      clearOverlay();
      RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed=false;

      if(width<4||height<4)return;
      const local={
        x:left/Math.max(1,r.width),
        y:top/Math.max(1,r.height),
        width:width/Math.max(1,r.width),
        height:height/Math.max(1,r.height)
      };
      const mapped=RenderFramework.mapDisplayedRegion(local);
      if(mapped)RenderFramework.renderRegion(mapped);
    };

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointerup',finishRegion);
    canvas?.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{
      drag=null;
      clearOverlay();
      RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed=false;
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('rfClear')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.clearFrame());

  });

  const syncAspect=source=>{

    if(source==='h'&&rw)rw.value=Math.max(16,Math.round((Number(rh.value)||720)*__renderAspectRatio));
  };
  rw?.addEventListener('change',()=>syncAspect('w'));
  rh?.addEventListener('change',()=>syncAspect('h'));
  ral?.addEventListener('change',()=>{__renderAspectRatio=Math.max(1e-6,(Number(rw?.value)||1280)/(Number(rh?.value)||720));});

  window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(e.target?.matches?.('input,select,textarea'))return;
    if(e.key==='F9'&&!e.shiftKey){
      e.preventDefault();RenderFramework.render();
    }else if(e.key==='F10'){
      e.preventDefault();RenderFramework.showSettings();
    }else if(e.key==='F9'&&e.shiftKey){

  });

const ThreeDLiteErrorBoundaryService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'boot3DLite',
  report(scope,error){
    const message=String(error?.message||error||'Unknown error');
    try{window.ThreeDLiteAppContext?.fail?.(scope,message);}catch(_e){}
    console.error(`[3DLite:${scope}]`,error);
    window.__3DLiteLastRuntimeError=Object.freeze({scope,message,time:Date.now()});
    return Object.freeze({scope,message});
  }
});

const ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'ArchitectureCore',
  run(){
    const health=ThreeDLiteHealthService.check();
    const renderer=ThreeDLiteValidationService.renderer();
    const errors=[];
    if(!health.ok)errors.push('architecture-health');
    if(renderer&&renderer.ok===false)errors.push('renderer-ownership');
    return Object.freeze({ok:errors.length===0,errors:Object.freeze(errors),health,renderer});
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService=ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService;

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

const RenderSettingsPolicy=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(s){
    const x={...s};
    x.engine=x.engine==='path'?'path':'fast';
    x.quality=['Draft','Preview','High','Ultra','Custom'].includes(x.quality)?x.quality:'Preview';
    x.width=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.width)||1280)));
    x.height=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.height)||720)));
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Math.min(200,Number(x.percentage)||100));
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.maxBounces)||5));
    x.background=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(x.background||'')?x.background:'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Math.min(1e6,Number(x.environmentStrength)||0));
    x.cpuThreads=x.cpuThreads==='Auto'?'Auto':String(Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.cpuThreads)||1)));
    x.tileSize=String(x.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(!['Auto','8','16','32','64'].includes(x.tileSize))x.tileSize='Auto';
    x.acceleration='BVH';
    x.progressive=x.progressive!==false;
    x.adaptive=x.adaptive!==false;
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Math.round(Number(x.minSamples)||8)));
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Math.min(4096,Math.round(Number(x.maxSamples)||64)));
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0.0001,Math.min(1,Number(x.noiseThreshold)||.03));
    x.denoise=['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(x.denoise)?x.denoise:'Off';
    x.toneMapping=['ACES','Reinhard','Linear'].includes(x.toneMapping)?x.toneMapping:'ACES';
    x.exposure=Math.max(-10,Math.min(10,Number(x.exposure)||0));
    x.primaryGI='Brute Force';x.secondaryGI=['None','Brute Force','Light Cache'].includes(x.secondaryGI)?x.secondaryGI:'Brute Force';
    x.format=x.format==='JPEG'?'JPEG':'PNG';
    x.colorDepth='8-bit';
    x.lightTree=!!x.lightTree;
    x.pathGuiding=!!x.pathGuiding;
    return Object.freeze(x);
  }
});

function applyRenderQualityPreset(name){
  const table={
    Draft:{bounces:2,min:2,max:8,noise:.08},
    Preview:{bounces:5,min:8,max:64,noise:.03},
    High:{bounces:8,min:16,max:256,noise:.015},
    Ultra:{bounces:12,min:32,max:512,noise:.008}
  };
  const p=table[name];if(!p)return;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v;};
  set('rsBounces',p.bounces);set('rsMinSamples',p.min);set('rsMaxSamples',p.max);set('rsNoise',p.noise);
}


function setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState(){
  const deferred=['rsPrimaryGI'];
  for(const id of deferred){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=true;el.title='Deferred to a later renderer group';}
  }
  for(const id of ['rsGI','rsSecondaryGI','rsBounces','rsProgressive','rsAdaptive','rsMinSamples','rsMaxSamples','rsNoise','rsDenoise','rsToneMap','rsExposure','rsLightTree','rsPathGuiding']){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=false;el.title='';}
  }
  const denoise=document.getElementById('rsDenoise');if(denoise&&!['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(denoise.value))denoise.value='Off';

  const format=document.getElementById('rsFormat');
  if(format){
    [...format.options].forEach(o=>{if(o.value==='EXR'||o.text==='EXR')o.disabled=true;});
    if(format.value==='EXR')format.value='PNG';
  }
  const depth=document.getElementById('rsDepth');
  if(depth){
    [...depth.options].forEach(o=>o.disabled=o.text!=='8-bit');
    depth.value='8-bit';
    depth.title='16/32-bit output is deferred until the render channel/export architecture is implemented';
  }
}

  {
    const canvas=document.getElementById('renderCanvas');
    let drag=null;
    let overlay=null;

    const clearOverlay=()=>{
      if(overlay)overlay.remove();
      overlay=null;
    };

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(!RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed)return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(rect.width,e.clientX-rect.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(rect.height,e.clientY-rect.top));
      drag={x,y,rect,pointerId:e.pointerId};

      clearOverlay();
      overlay=document.createElement('div');
      overlay.style.cssText='position:fixed;border:1px dashed #fff;pointer-events:none;z-index:100000;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #000;';
      document.body.appendChild(overlay);
      canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
    });

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||!overlay)return;
      const r=drag.rect;
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.width,e.clientX-r.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.height,e.clientY-r.top));
      const left=Math.min(drag.x,x);
      const top=Math.min(drag.y,y);
      const width=Math.abs(x-drag.x);
      const height=Math.abs(y-drag.y);

      overlay.style.left=(r.left+left)+'px';
      overlay.style.top=(r.top+top)+'px';
      overlay.style.width=width+'px';
      overlay.style.height=height+'px';
    });

    const finishRegion=e=>{
      if(!drag)return;
      const r=drag.rect;
      const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.width,e.clientX-r.left));
      const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(r.height,e.clientY-r.top));
      const left=Math.min(drag.x,x);
      const top=Math.min(drag.y,y);
      const width=Math.abs(x-drag.x);
      const height=Math.abs(y-drag.y);

      drag=null;
      clearOverlay();
      RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed=false;

      if(width<4||height<4)return;
      const local={
        x:left/Math.max(1,r.width),
        y:top/Math.max(1,r.height),
        width:width/Math.max(1,r.width),
        height:height/Math.max(1,r.height)
      };
      const mapped=RenderFramework.mapDisplayedRegion(local);
      if(mapped)RenderFramework.renderRegion(mapped);
    };

    canvas?.addEventListener('pointerup',finishRegion);
    canvas?.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{
      drag=null;
      clearOverlay();
      RenderFramework.regionSelectArmed=false;
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('rfClear')?.addEventListener('click',()=>RenderFramework.clearFrame());

  const rw=document.getElementById('rsWidth'),rh=document.getElementById('rsHeight'),ral=document.getElementById('rsAspectLock');
  const syncAspect=source=>{
    if(!ral?.checked){__renderAspectRatio=Math.max(1e-6,(Number(rw?.value)||1280)/(Number(rh?.value)||720));return;}
    if(source==='w'&&rh)rh.value=Math.max(16,Math.round((Number(rw.value)||1280)/__renderAspectRatio));
    if(source==='h'&&rw)rw.value=Math.max(16,Math.round((Number(rh.value)||720)*__renderAspectRatio));
  };
  rw?.addEventListener('change',()=>syncAspect('w'));
  rh?.addEventListener('change',()=>syncAspect('h'));
  ral?.addEventListener('change',()=>{__renderAspectRatio=Math.max(1e-6,(Number(rw?.value)||1280)/(Number(rh?.value)||720));});

  window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(e.target?.matches?.('input,select,textarea'))return;
    if(e.key==='F9'&&!e.shiftKey){
      e.preventDefault();RenderFramework.render();
    }else if(e.key==='F10'){
      e.preventDefault();RenderFramework.showSettings();
    }else if(e.key==='F9'&&e.shiftKey){
      e.preventDefault();RenderFramework.renderLast();
    }
  },true);

  RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();
  RenderFramework.initialized=true;
  window.RenderFramework=RenderFramework;
  return true;
}

function runRendererConsolidationChecks(){
  const failures=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)failures.push(m);};
  try{
    expect(RendererMasterCore.createRequest.toString().includes('RenderCore1.createRequest'),'Master request bypasses Core 1');
    expect(RendererMasterCore.prepare.toString().includes('RenderCore1.createExecution'),'Master prepare bypasses Core 1 execution');
    expect(RendererMasterCore.prepare.toString().includes('RenderCore2.compileShading'),'Master prepare bypasses Core 2 shading');
    expect(RenderCore1.createRequest.toString().includes('RenderCoreA.createRequest'),'Core 1 does not delegate to Core A');
    expect(!RenderCore1.createRequest.toString().includes('RendererMasterCore.createRequest'),'Core 1 recursion reintroduced');
    expect(RenderFramework.render.toString().includes('RendererMasterCore.createRequest'),'RenderFramework bypasses Master Core');
    expect(typeof RenderPart2Core==='object'&&typeof RenderPart3Core==='object'&&typeof RenderPart4Core==='object'&&typeof RenderPart5Core==='object'&&typeof RenderPart6Core==='object','Part Core boundary missing');
    expect(typeof selected!=='undefined','authoritative selected variable unavailable');
    expect(typeof syncTransformTarget==='function','authoritative transform synchronization API missing');
  }catch(err){failures.push('Consolidation exception: '+String(err?.message||err));}
    if(!/RenderCoreE\.prepareShading/.test(RendererMasterCore.prepare.toString()))failures.push('Master Core does not route shading through Core E');
  if(!/RenderCoreE\.rendererFor/.test(RendererMasterCore.rendererFor.toString()))failures.push('Master Core does not route renderer selection through Core E');
  if(typeof RenderGroup11Core?.create!=='function')failures.push('Group 11 Light Cache factory missing');
  if(!/LightCacheEstimator/.test(PathIntegrator.trace.toString()))failures.push('Path Integrator does not consume Group 11 Light Cache');
  if(!/secondaryGI/.test(PathIntegrator.trace.toString()))failures.push('Secondary GI mode boundary missing');
return {ok:failures.length===0,failures};
}
window.runRendererConsolidationChecks=runRendererConsolidationChecks;

class RenderJob{
  constructor(id,settings,mode='full',region=null){
    this.id=id;
    this.settings=settings;
    this.mode=mode;
    this.region=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.cancelled=false;this.paused=false;this.finished=false;this.error=null;
    this.startedAt=0;this.finishedAt=0;this.renderStartedAt=0;
    this.progress=new RenderProgressState();
    this.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    this.controlToken16=new RenderControlToken16();
    this.setCancelled=v=>{this.cancelled=!!v;if(v)this.controlToken16.cancel();};
    this.setPaused=v=>{this.paused=!!v;v?this.controlToken16.pause():this.controlToken16.resume();};
  }
}

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

  version:'3.79.16-part2-core',

  },

  },
  ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height){return RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height);},

  }

const RenderCore1=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-1',
  createRequest(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    try{
      const job=RenderCoreA.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RendererRuntimeState.snapshotReady=!!job?.renderScene;
      return job;
    }catch(err){
      RendererRuntimeState.snapshotReady=false;
      throw err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);
    }
  },
  createExecution(job){
    if(!job?.renderScene)throw new SnapshotError('Missing immutable RenderSceneSnapshot');
    try{return {job,geometry:RenderCoreB.createExecutionGeometry(job.renderScene)};}
    catch(err){throw err instanceof RendererError?err:new GeometryCompileError(String(err?.message||err),err);}
  },
  async prepareExecution(execution,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    try{execution.prepared=await RenderCoreB.prepareWorkerGeometry(execution.job,pool,execution.geometry,onProgress);return execution;}
    catch(err){throw err instanceof RendererError?err:new WorkerError(String(err?.message||err),err);}
  },
  buildAcceleration(execution){
    try{execution.acceleration=RenderCoreB.buildAccelerationFromPackets(execution.geometry.accelerationPackets);RendererRuntimeState.accelerationReady=true;return execution;}
    catch(err){RendererRuntimeState.accelerationReady=false;throw new BVHError(String(err?.message||err),err);}
  },
  validate(){const errors=[];const a=RenderCoreA.validate(),b=RenderCoreB.validate(),p3=validateRenderPart3();if(!a.ok)errors.push(...a.errors);if(!b.ok)errors.push(...b.errors);if(!p3.ok)errors.push(...p3.errors);return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};}
});

const RendererMasterCore=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-master-core',
  createRequest(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    return RenderCore1.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
  },
  async prepare(job,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    let t=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    let e=RenderCore1.createExecution(job);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'geometry',t);

    t=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    e=await RenderCore1.prepareExecution(e,pool,onProgress);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'worker',t);

    t=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    e=RenderCore1.buildAcceleration(e);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'bvh',t);

    t=performance.now();
    const baseShading=RenderCore2.compileShading(job);
    e.shading=RenderCoreE.prepareShading(job,e.acceleration,baseShading);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'shading',t);
    return e;
  },
  rendererFor(job){return RenderCoreE.rendererFor(job);},
  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const a=RenderCore1.validate(),b=RenderCore2.validate(),e=RenderCoreE.validate(),p6=validateRenderPart6();
    if(!a.ok)errors.push(...a.errors);
    if(!b.ok)errors.push(...b.errors);
    if(!e.ok)errors.push(...e.errors);
    if(!p6.ok)errors.push(...p6.errors);
    if(typeof RenderCore1.createRequest!=='function')errors.push('Core 1 request API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCore1.createExecution!=='function')errors.push('Core 1 execution API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCore2.compileShading!=='function')errors.push('Core 2 shading API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCoreE.prepareShading!=='function'||typeof RenderCoreE.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Core E API missing');
    if(typeof this.prepare!=='function'||typeof this.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Master Core orchestration API missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){

    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;
    job.lightTree=RenderCoreH.createLightTree(job,compiled);
    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;

    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const primaryMask=new Uint8Array(width*height);

    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();

    const maxSamples=Math.max(1,settings.maxSamples||1);
    const progressive=settings.progressive!==false;
    const passLimit=progressive?maxSamples:1;
    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));

      let sampledThisPass=0;
      const activePixels16=progressive?accumulator.compactActive(settings):(()=>{

      })();

      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;

          const jitterY=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,21)-.5;
          const ray=RenderPart5Core.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x+jitterX,rr.y0+y+jitterY,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){
            const ph=sample.primaryHitRecord;
            if(ph?.hit)RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,ph,seed));
            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();
      onProgress(Math.min(1,completedPasses/passLimit));

      if(!progressive)break;
      if(settings.adaptive&&completedPasses>=settings.minSamples&&accumulator.active===0)break;
      if(sampledThisPass===0)break;
    }

    const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,accumulator.samples[i]),o=i*3;
      const beauty=[accumulator.sum[o]/s,accumulator.sum[o+1]/s,accumulator.sum[o+2]/s];
      aovs.set3('Beauty',i,beauty);
      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');
      const direct=d?[d[o],d[o+1],d[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      const emission=e?[e[o],e[o+1],e[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      aovs.set3('Global Illumination',i,[
        Math.max(0,beauty[0]-direct[0]-emission[0]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[1]-direct[1]-emission[1]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[2]-direct[2]-emission[2])
      ]);
    }
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
        samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
        passes:completedPasses,
        maxBounces:PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job),
        estimator:'Path GI + direct NEE + power-heuristic MIS',
        mis:true,
        adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
        progressive:!!settings.progressive,
        russianRoulette:true,
        importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture',
        noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),

        secondaryGI:settings.secondaryGI,
        lightCache:job.lightCache?.stats?.()||null,
        emissiveTriangleLights:compiled.emissiveTriangles?.triangles?.length||0,
        diagnostics:RenderPart5Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }

const RenderGroup8Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'multi-bounce path integration',
    'path throughput',
    'secondary-ray spawning',
    'cosine hemisphere BSDF sampling baseline',
    'environment contribution on path miss',
    'path-GI render mode'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B ray/BVH tracing',
    'Core C physical materials and lights',
    'Group 7 shadow visibility and direct-light sampling'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'progressive accumulation',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'MIS',
    'Light Cache',

  ])

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  PathSampler,PathBSDFSampler,PathIntegrator,PathGIRenderer,RenderGroup8Contract
});

function runRenderGroup9SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const w=RenderMIS.power(.5,.5);
    expect(Math.abs(w-.5)<1e-9,'MIS equal-PDF power heuristic failed');
    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=new ProgressiveAccumulator(2,1);
    a.add(0,[1,1,1]);a.add(0,[1,1,1]);
    a.add(1,[0,0,0]);a.add(1,[2,2,2]);
    expect(a.samples[0]===2&&a.samples[1]===2,'Accumulator sample count failed');
    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      progressive:true,adaptive:true,minSamples:4,maxSamples:32,noiseThreshold:.02
    });
    expect(s.progressive&&s.adaptive,'Sampling toggles lost');
    expect(s.minSamples===4&&s.maxSamples===32&&Math.abs(s.noiseThreshold-.02)<1e-9,'Sampling settings lost');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Sam

err)}`);}

    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};

  initialize(){
    RendererRuntimeState.reset();

      initRenderGroup1();
      RendererRuntimeState.framebufferReady=!!RenderFramework.ui?.frameBuffer;
      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=!!RenderFramework.jobs?.workerPool;
      const r=this.validate();
      if(!r.ok){
        r.errors.forEach(e=>RendererRuntimeState.fail('validation',new RendererError('validation',e)));
        return r;
      }
      RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
      return {ok:true,errors:[]};
    }catch(err){
      RendererRuntimeState.fail('bootstrap',err);
      return {ok:false,errors:[String(err?.message||err)]};
    }
  },
  showFailure(error){
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML='Renderer unavailable.<br>'+String(error?.message||error);}
  },
  recover(){
    try{
      RenderFramework.jobs?.cancel?.(false);
      if(RenderFramework.jobs?.workerPool){RenderPart3Core.recover(RenderFramework.jobs);RenderFramework.jobs.configureThreads(RenderFramework.settings.cpuThreads);}
      const r=this.validate();
      if(!r.ok){r.errors.forEach(e=>RendererRuntimeState.fail('recovery-validation',new RendererError('recovery-validation',e)));return r;}
      const release=(typeof runRendererReleaseGate==='function')?runRendererReleaseGate():{ok:true,failures:[]};
      window.__3DLiteRendererRecoveryWarnings=release.ok?[]:[...(release.failures||[])];
      if(!release.ok){
        console.warn('[3DLite Renderer] Recovery release diagnostics reported warnings:',release.failures);
      }
      RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');if(notice){notice.style.display='none';notice.innerHTML='';}
      return {ok:true,errors:[]};
    }catch(err){RendererRuntimeState.fail('recovery',err);return {ok:false,errors:[String(err?.message||err)]};}
  },
  shutdown(){try{RenderFramework.jobs?.dispose?.();}catch(_){}RendererRuntimeState.reset();}
});
