const RenderJobStatus=Object.freeze({
  IDLE:'Idle',
  PREPARING:'Preparing',
  PREPARING_GEOMETRY:'Preparing Geometry',
  RENDERING:'Rendering',
  PAUSED:'Paused',
  FINISHED:'Finished',
  CANCELLED:'Cancelled',
  ERROR:'Error'
});

class RenderSettingsModel{
  constructor(){
    this.engine='fast';
    this.quality='Preview';
    this.width=1280;this.height=720;this.percentage=100;
    this.aspectLock=true;this.transparent=false;
    this.format='PNG';this.colorDepth='8-bit';
    this.camera='Active View';this.focalLength=50;this.sensorWidth=36;
    this.fStop=8;this.iso=100;
    this.globalIllumination=true;this.primaryGI='Brute Force';this.secondaryGI='Brute Force';
    this.progressive=true;this.adaptive=true;this.minSamples=8;this.maxSamples=64;this.noiseThreshold=.03;
    this.maxBounces=5;this.denoise='Off';this.toneMapping='ACES';this.exposure=0;
    this.background='#202020';this.environmentStrength=1;
    this.cpuThreads='Auto';this.tileSize='Auto';this.acceleration='BVH';
    this.lightTree=false;this.pathGuiding=false;
  }
  clone(){return Object.freeze({...this});}
}

class RenderProgressState{
  constructor(){
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.percent=0;this.currentSamples=0;this.maximumSamples=0;
    this.noise=null;this.threads=0;this.resolution=[0,0];this.gi='—';
    this.elapsedMs=0;this.raysPerSecond=null;this.bvhBuildMs=null;
  }
  snapshot(){return Object.freeze({...this,resolution:Object.freeze([...this.resolution])});}
}

class RenderJob{
  constructor(id,settings,mode='full',region=null){
    this.id=id;
    this.settings=settings;
    this.mode=mode;
    this.region=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.cancelled=false;this.paused=false;this.finished=false;this.error=null;
    this.startedAt=0;this.finishedAt=0;this.renderStartedAt=0;
    this.progress=new RenderProgressState();
    this.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    this.controlToken16=new RenderControlToken16();
    this.setCancelled=v=>{this.cancelled=!!v;if(v)this.controlToken16.cancel();};
    this.setPaused=v=>{this.paused=!!v;v?this.controlToken16.pause():this.controlToken16.resume();};
  }
}

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

var RenderPerformanceMetrics=Object.freeze({
  begin(job){job.startedAt=performance.now();job.finishedAt=0;return job.startedAt;},
  beginTracing(job){job.renderStartedAt=performance.now();},
  finish(job){
    job.finishedAt=performance.now();
    job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,job.finishedAt-(job.startedAt||job.finishedAt));
    const seconds=Math.max(1e-6,(job.finishedAt-(job.renderStartedAt||job.startedAt||job.finishedAt))/1000);
    const measured=RenderCoreH.performance(job);
    const actualRays=Math.max(0,Number(measured?.totalRays)||0);
    job.progress.raysPerSecond=actualRays?Math.round(actualRays/seconds):0;
    job.performance=Object.freeze({
      totalMs:job.progress.elapsedMs,
      traceMs:seconds*1000,
      actualRays,
      raysPerSecond:job.progress.raysPerSecond,
      samples:Number(measured?.samples)||0,
      yields:Number(measured?.yields)||0,
      bvhBuildMs:job.progress.bvhBuildMs
    });
    return job.performance;
  }
});

var ViewportDebugLine=Object.freeze({
  element(){return document.getElementById('viewportDebugText');},
  set(message){
    const el=this.element();
    if(el)el.textContent=String(message??'');
    return String(message??'');
  },
  renderer(){
    let state='unknown';
    try{
      const snap=RendererRuntimeState?.snapshot?.();
      state=snap?.degraded?'degraded':(snap?.rendererReady?'ready':'not-ready');
    }catch(_){}
    const job=RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;
    const diag=LiteTraceDiagnostics.snapshot(job);
    const perf=diag.performance?.performance||RenderCoreH?.performance?.(job)||null;
    const workers=diag.performance?.workers||RenderFramework?.jobs?.workerStats?.()||null;
    const tile=job?RenderCoreH?.tileSize?.(job):null;
    const engine=job?.settings?.engine||'—';
    const rays=perf?.totalRays||0;
    const rps=perf?.raysPerSecond||job?.progress?.raysPerSecond||0;
    const samples=perf?.samples||job?.progress?.currentSamples||0;
    const renderMs=Math.round(perf?.stageMs?.render||0);
    return this.set(
      `LiteTrace ${LiteTrace?.version||'1.0 RC2'} | 3DLite v${ThreeDLiteVersion.version} | ${engine}`+
      ` | renderer=${state}`+
      ` | workers=${workers?.workers??0}/${workers?.configuredSize??0}`+
      ` | tile=${tile??'—'}`+
      ` | rays=${rays}`+
      ` | rps=${rps}`+
      ` | samples=${samples}`+
      ` | render=${renderMs}ms`+
      ` | viewport=${document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady||'false'}`+
      ` | error=${document.documentElement.dataset.rendererLastError||'none'}`
    );
  },
  error(error,stage='runtime'){
    const msg=String(error?.message||error||'unknown error').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    return this.set(`3DLite DEBUG | stage=${stage} | ${msg}`);
  },
  async copy(){
    const text=this.element()?.textContent||'';
    try{
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    }catch(_){
      const ta=document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value=text;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';
      document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();
      try{document.execCommand('copy');}catch(__){}
      ta.remove();
    }
    return text;
  }
});

let __3DLiteBooted=false;

function boot3DLite(){
  ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.EDITOR_BOOTING,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.EDITOR);
  register3DLiteCoreServices();
  const serviceDeps=validate3DLiteServiceDependencies();
  if(!serviceDeps.ok)console.warn('[3DLite] service dependency warnings:',serviceDeps.missing);
  if(__3DLiteBooted||window.__3DLiteBooting)return;

  const required=[
    ['THREE',window.THREE],
    ['OrbitControls',window.THREE?.OrbitControls],
    ['TransformControls',window.THREE?.TransformControls]
  ];
  const missing=required.filter(([,v])=>!v).map(([n])=>n);
  if(missing.length){
    window.__3DLiteBooted=false;
    window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Missing required viewport dependency: '+missing.join(', '));
    return;
  }

  window.__3DLiteBooting=true;
  document.documentElement.dataset.editorState='booting';
  document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady='false';
  document.documentElement.dataset.rendererReady='false';

  EditorContext=new EditorContextClass();
  window.EditorContext=EditorContext;
  const arch=validateArchitectureCore();
  if(!arch.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Architecture validation failed: '+arch.errors.join(', '));return;}
  const archTests=runArchitectureSelfTests();
  if(!archTests.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Architecture self-test failed: '+archTests.failures.join(', '));return;}
  const transformValidation=validateTransformSystem();
  if(!transformValidation.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Transform validation failed: '+transformValidation.errors.join(', '));return;}
  const transformTests=runTransformSystemSelfTests();
  if(!transformTests.ok){window.__3DLiteBooting=false;window.__3DLiteShowStartupError?.('Transform self-test failed: '+transformTests.failures.join(', '));return;}
  document.documentElement.dataset.architectureSelfTest='passed';
  document.documentElement.dataset.transformSystemSelfTest='passed';
  refreshModifyPanel();
  resize();
  if(typeof resizeViewportToLayout==='function')resizeViewportToLayout();
  requestRender();
  requestStatsUpdate();
  document.documentElement.dataset.viewportReady='true';
  document.documentElement.dataset.editorState='ready';
  ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.EDITOR_READY,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.VIEWPORT);
  window.__3DLiteBootRenderReady=true;

  // LiteTrace starts after the modeling viewport is ready; renderer failure is fail-soft.
  queueMicrotask(()=>{
    ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.RENDERER_BOOTING,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.RENDERER);
    try{
      const rendererBoot=RendererBootstrap.initialize();
      if(!rendererBoot.ok)RendererBootstrap.showFailure(new RendererError('bootstrap',rendererBoot.errors.join('; ')));
      const rendererRelease=runRendererReleaseGate();
      window.__3DLiteRendererReleaseCandidate=rendererRelease.ok;
      window.__3DLiteRendererReleaseWarnings=rendererRelease.ok?[]:[...(rendererRelease.failures||[])];
      if(!rendererRelease.ok)console.warn('[LiteTrace] Non-blocking release-gate warnings:',rendererRelease.failures);
      ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(
        rendererBoot.ok?ThreeDLiteLifecycle.READY:ThreeDLiteLifecycle.DEGRADED,
        ThreeDLiteBootPhase.READY
      );
      ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();
    }catch(err){
      ThreeDLiteAppContext.fail('renderer-bootstrap',err);
      ThreeDLiteAppContext.setLifecycle(ThreeDLiteLifecycle.DEGRADED,ThreeDLiteBootPhase.READY);
      console.error('[LiteTrace] deferred initialization failed',err);
      try{ViewportDebugLine?.error?.(err,'renderer-bootstrap');}catch(_){}
    }
  });
  if(!window.__3DLiteAnimationLoopStarted){window.__3DLiteAnimationLoopStarted=true;animate(performance.now());}

  __3DLiteBooted=true;
  window.__3DLiteBooted=true;
  window.__3DLiteBooting=false;
  document.documentElement.dataset.editorBooted='true';
  ViewportDebugLine.renderer();
  document.documentElement.dataset.architectureCore='3.78.28-gen3-transform-system';
  document.documentElement.dataset.transformSystem='3.78.28-gen3';
  document.documentElement.dataset.blenderMaxIntegrated='true';
}

  const state={version:VERSION,reports:[],active:null,last:null,lastCode:null,monitor:null};

  function classify(errorOrMessage,stage='render'){
    const msg=String(errorOrMessage?.message||errorOrMessage||'').toLowerCase();
    const st=String(errorOrMessage?.stage||stage||'render').toLowerCase();
    if(/material index out of range/.test(msg))return 'RND-0501';
    if(/missing material/.test(msg))return 'RND-0502';
    if(/texture/.test(msg)&&/missing|not found|unavailable|failed/.test(msg))return 'RND-0601';
    if(/nan|infinity|non-finite|nonfinite/.test(msg))return 'RND-0801';
    if(/out of memory|allocation|resource exhausted|gpu memory/.test(msg))return 'RND-1001';
    if(/reflection/.test(msg)&&/recurs|bounce|depth|limit/.test(msg))return 'RND-0301';
    if(/refraction|transmission/.test(msg)&&/recurs|bounce|depth|limit/.test(msg))return 'RND-0302';
    if(/ray/.test(msg)&&/count|budget|limit|excess/.test(msg))return 'RND-0303';
    if(/self[- ]?intersect/.test(msg))return 'RND-0202';
    if(/bvh|intersection/.test(msg))return 'RND-0203';
    if(/invalid ray|ray.*nan|ray.*infinity/.test(msg))return 'RND-0201';
    if(/shadow/.test(msg)&&/noise|noisy/.test(msg))return 'RND-0401';
    if(/\bgi\b|global illumination/.test(msg)&&/noise|noisy/.test(msg))return 'RND-0402';
    if(/denois/.test(msg))return 'RND-1301';
    if(/composit/.test(msg))return 'RND-0902';
    if(/pass/.test(msg))return 'RND-0901';
    if(st==='snapshot'||/snapshot/.test(msg))return 'RND-1101';
    if(st==='geometry'||/geometry compile/.test(msg))return 'RND-1102';
    if(st==='worker'||/worker/.test(msg))return 'RND-1103';
    if(st==='render'||st==='execution')return 'RND-1104';
    return 'RND-0001';
  }

  function jobEvidence(job){
    const p=job?.progress||{};
    const ps=job?.performanceState||job?.performance||{};
    return {
      renderId:job?.id??null,
      mode:job?.mode??null,
      status:job?.status??p.status??null,
      progressPercent:num(p.percent),
      currentSamples:num(p.currentSamples),
      maximumSamples:num(p.maximumSamples),
      resolution:Array.isArray(p.resolution)?p.resolution.slice(0,2):null,
      elapsedMs:num(p.elapsedMs),
      raysPerSecond:num(p.raysPerSecond??ps.raysPerSecond??ps.estimatedRaysPerSecond),
      bvhBuildMs:num(p.bvhBuildMs??ps.bvhBuildMs),
      raysCast:num(ps.rays??ps.rayCount??ps.totalRays),
      primaryRays:num(ps.primaryRays),secondaryRays:num(ps.secondaryRays),shadowRays:num(ps.shadowRays),
      reflectionRays:num(ps.reflectionRays),refractionRays:num(ps.refractionRays),
      intersections:num(ps.intersections),misses:num(ps.misses),bvhTests:num(ps.bvhTests),
      invalidRays:num(ps.invalidRays),bounceDepth:num(ps.maxBounce??ps.bounceDepth),noise:num(p.noise),
      engine:job?.settings?.engine??null,maxBounces:num(job?.settings?.maxBounces),
      error:job?.error?String(job.error.message||job.error):null,errorStage:job?.error?.stage||null
    };
  }

  function learn(report){
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NDebugRegistry?.register?.({
      category:CODES[report.code]?.category||'rendering',subtype:report.code.toLowerCase(),severity:report.severity,
      subsystem:'l3n-rendering-debugger',source:'render-debugger',message:report.code+' '+report.name+(report.message?': '+report.message:''),
      reproduced:true,regression:report.severity==='critical'||report.severity==='high',evidence:report,status:report.status||'open'
    });}catch(_){}
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NTargetLearning?.record?.(15,'learning','render debugger '+report.code+' '+report.name);}catch(_){}
  }

  function record(code='RND-0001',input={}){
    if(!CODES[code])code='RND-0001';
    const meta=CODES[code];
    const report={schema:1,version:VERSION,code,name:meta.name,severity:String(input.severity||meta.severity),category:meta.category,
      detectedAt:now(),renderId:input.renderId??input.evidence?.renderId??state.active?.renderId??null,
      stage:String(input.stage||input.evidence?.errorStage||'render'),message:String(input.message||''),status:String(input.status||'open'),
      evidence:safe(input.evidence||{}),recommendedAction:String(input.recommendedAction||''),lastSuccessfulStage:String(input.lastSuccessfulStage||'')};
    state.reports.push(report);if(state.reports.length>MAX_REPORTS)state.reports.shift();state.last=report;state.lastCode=code;learn(report);renderPanel();return Object.freeze({...report});
  }

  function begin(job){
    state.active={renderId:job?.id??null,startedAt:perf(),startedWall:now(),lastProgressAt:perf(),lastPercent:num(job?.progress?.percent)||0,lastStatus:String(job?.status||''),stallReported:false,freezeReported:false};
    state.lastCode=null;renderPanel();
  }
  function finish(job){
    if(!state.active||state.active.renderId!==(job?.id??null))return;
    state.last={schema:1,version:VERSION,code:null,name:'Render completed',severity:'info',category:'rendering',detectedAt:now(),renderId:job?.id??null,stage:'complete',message:'Render completed successfully',status:'resolved',evidence:jobEvidence(job)};
    state.active=null;renderPanel();
  }
  function fail(job){
    const e=job?.error;const code=classify(e,e?.stage||'render');
    record(code,{renderId:job?.id,stage:e?.stage||'render',message:String(e?.message||e||'Render failed'),evidence:jobEvidence(job),lastSuccessfulStage:state.active?.lastStatus||''});
    state.active=null;
  }

  function tick(){
    const job=getActive();
    if(job&&!state.active)begin(job);
    if(!job){renderPanel();return;}
    if(state.active&&state.active.renderId!==(job.id??null))begin(job);
    const a=state.active;if(!a)return;
    const status=String(job.status||job.progress?.status||'');
    const pct=num(job.progress?.percent)||0;
    if(pct>a.lastPercent+0.0001||status!==a.lastStatus){a.lastPercent=pct;a.lastStatus=status;a.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    const age=perf()-a.lastProgressAt;
    if(/rendering/i.test(status)&&age>=STALL_MS&&!a.stallReported){
      a.stallReported=true;record('RND-0101',{renderId:job.id,stage:'render',message:'Render made no measurable progress for '+Math.round(age)+' ms',evidence:jobEvidence(job),recommendedAction:'Inspect current pass, ray counts, recursion and material/light state.'});
    }
    if(job.error||/error/i.test(status)){fail(job);return;}
    if(job.finished||/finished|complete/i.test(status)){finish(job);return;}
    renderPanel();
  }

  function snapshot(){return Object.freeze({version:VERSION,codes:CODES,active:safe(state.active),last:safe(state.last),lastCode:state.lastCode,reports:Object.freeze(state.reports.map(r=>Object.freeze({...r})))});}
  function clear(){state.reports.length=0;state.last=null;state.lastCode=null;renderPanel();return true;}
  function exportReport(){return JSON.stringify(snapshot(),null,2);}

  function ensureUI(){

  function classify(errorOrMessage,stage='render'){
    const msg=String(errorOrMessage?.message||errorOrMessage||'').toLowerCase();
    const st=String(errorOrMessage?.stage||stage||'render').toLowerCase();
    if(/material index out of range/.test(msg))return 'RND-0501';
    if(/missing material/.test(msg))return 'RND-0502';
    if(/texture/.test(msg)&&/missing|not found|unavailable|failed/.test(msg))return 'RND-0601';
    if(/nan|infinity|non-finite|nonfinite/.test(msg))return 'RND-0801';
    if(/out of memory|allocation|resource exhausted|gpu memory/.test(msg))return 'RND-1001';
    if(/reflection/.test(msg)&&/recurs|bounce|depth|limit/.test(msg))return 'RND-0301';
    if(/refraction|transmission/.test(msg)&&/recurs|bounce|depth|limit/.test(msg))return 'RND-0302';
    if(/ray/.test(msg)&&/count|budget|limit|excess/.test(msg))return 'RND-0303';
    if(/self[- ]?intersect/.test(msg))return 'RND-0202';
    if(/bvh|intersection/.test(msg))return 'RND-0203';
    if(/invalid ray|ray.*nan|ray.*infinity/.test(msg))return 'RND-0201';
    if(/shadow/.test(msg)&&/noise|noisy/.test(msg))return 'RND-0401';
    if(/\bgi\b|global illumination/.test(msg)&&/noise|noisy/.test(msg))return 'RND-0402';
    if(/denois/.test(msg))return 'RND-1301';
    if(/composit/.test(msg))return 'RND-0902';
    if(/pass/.test(msg))return 'RND-0901';
    if(st==='snapshot'||/snapshot/.test(msg))return 'RND-1101';
    if(st==='geometry'||/geometry compile/.test(msg))return 'RND-1102';
    if(st==='worker'||/worker/.test(msg))return 'RND-1103';
    if(st==='render'||st==='execution')return 'RND-1104';
    return 'RND-0001';
  }

  function jobEvidence(job){
    const p=job?.progress||{};
    const ps=job?.performanceState||job?.performance||{};
    return {
      renderId:job?.id??null,
      mode:job?.mode??null,
      status:job?.status??p.status??null,
      progressPercent:num(p.percent),
      currentSamples:num(p.currentSamples),
      maximumSamples:num(p.maximumSamples),
      resolution:Array.isArray(p.resolution)?p.resolution.slice(0,2):null,
      elapsedMs:num(p.elapsedMs),
      raysPerSecond:num(p.raysPerSecond??ps.raysPerSecond??ps.estimatedRaysPerSecond),
      bvhBuildMs:num(p.bvhBuildMs??ps.bvhBuildMs),
      raysCast:num(ps.rays??ps.rayCount??ps.totalRays),
      primaryRays:num(ps.primaryRays),secondaryRays:num(ps.secondaryRays),shadowRays:num(ps.shadowRays),
      reflectionRays:num(ps.reflectionRays),refractionRays:num(ps.refractionRays),
      intersections:num(ps.intersections),misses:num(ps.misses),bvhTests:num(ps.bvhTests),
      invalidRays:num(ps.invalidRays),bounceDepth:num(ps.maxBounce??ps.bounceDepth),noise:num(p.noise),
      engine:job?.settings?.engine??null,maxBounces:num(job?.settings?.maxBounces),
      error:job?.error?String(job.error.message||job.error):null,errorStage:job?.error?.stage||null
    };
  }

  function learn(report){
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NDebugRegistry?.register?.({
      category:CODES[report.code]?.category||'rendering',subtype:report.code.toLowerCase(),severity:report.severity,
      subsystem:'l3n-rendering-debugger',source:'render-debugger',message:report.code+' '+report.name+(report.message?': '+report.message:''),
      reproduced:true,regression:report.severity==='critical'||report.severity==='high',evidence:report,status:report.status||'open'
    });}catch(_){}
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NTargetLearning?.record?.(15,'learning','render debugger '+report.code+' '+report.name);}catch(_){}
  }

  function record(code='RND-0001',input={}){
    if(!CODES[code])code='RND-0001';
    const meta=CODES[code];
    const report={schema:1,version:VERSION,code,name:meta.name,severity:String(input.severity||meta.severity),category:meta.category,
      detectedAt:now(),renderId:input.renderId??input.evidence?.renderId??state.active?.renderId??null,
      stage:String(input.stage||input.evidence?.errorStage||'render'),message:String(input.message||''),status:String(input.status||'open'),
      evidence:safe(input.evidence||{}),recommendedAction:String(input.recommendedAction||''),lastSuccessfulStage:String(input.lastSuccessfulStage||'')};
    state.reports.push(report);if(state.reports.length>MAX_REPORTS)state.reports.shift();state.last=report;state.lastCode=code;learn(report);renderPanel();return Object.freeze({...report});
  }

  function begin(job){
    state.active={renderId:job?.id??null,startedAt:perf(),startedWall:now(),lastProgressAt:perf(),lastPercent:num(job?.progress?.percent)||0,lastStatus:String(job?.status||''),stallReported:false,freezeReported:false};
    state.lastCode=null;renderPanel();
  }
  function finish(job){
    if(!state.active||state.active.renderId!==(job?.id??null))return;
    state.last={schema:1,version:VERSION,code:null,name:'Render completed',severity:'info',category:'rendering',detectedAt:now(),renderId:job?.id??null,stage:'complete',message:'Render completed successfully',status:'resolved',evidence:jobEvidence(job)};
    state.active=null;renderPanel();
  }
  function fail(job){
    const e=job?.error;const code=classify(e,e?.stage||'render');
    record(code,{renderId:job?.id,stage:e?.stage||'render',message:String(e?.message||e||'Render failed'),evidence:jobEvidence(job),lastSuccessfulStage:state.active?.lastStatus||''});
    state.active=null;
  }

  function tick(){
    const job=getActive();
    if(job&&!state.active)begin(job);
    if(!job){renderPanel();return;}
    if(state.active&&state.active.renderId!==(job.id??null))begin(job);
    const a=state.active;if(!a)return;
    const status=String(job.status||job.progress?.status||'');
    const pct=num(job.progress?.percent)||0;
    if(pct>a.lastPercent+0.0001||status!==a.lastStatus){a.lastPercent=pct;a.lastStatus=status;a.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    const age=perf()-a.lastProgressAt;
    if(/rendering/i.test(status)&&age>=STALL_MS&&!a.stallReported){
      a.stallReported=true;record('RND-0101',{renderId:job.id,stage:'render',message:'Render made no measurable progress for '+Math.round(age)+' ms',evidence:jobEvidence(job),recommendedAction:'Inspect current pass, ray counts, recursion and material/light state.'});
    }
    if(job.error||/error/i.test(status)){fail(job);return;}
    if(job.finished||/finished|complete/i.test(status)){finish(job);return;}
    renderPanel();
  }

  function snapshot(){return Object.freeze({version:VERSION,codes:CODES,active:safe(state.active),last:safe(state.last),lastCode:state.lastCode,reports:Object.freeze(state.reports.map(r=>Object.freeze({...r})))});}
  function clear(){state.reports.length=0;state.last=null;state.lastCode=null;renderPanel();return true;}
  function exportReport(){return JSON.stringify(snapshot(),null,2);}

  const safe=v=>{try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));}catch(_){return null;}};

  function pushEvent(e){state.events.push(e);if(state.events.length>MAX_EVENTS)state.events.shift();return e;}
  function pushSample(x){state.samples.push(x);if(state.samples.length>MAX_SAMPLES)state.samples.shift();return x;}
  function job(){return window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;}
  function jobEvidence(j=job()){
    const b=base();

    const p=j?.progress||{};
    const ps=j?.performanceState||j?.performance||{};
    return {...safe(core),
      renderId:j?.id??core.renderId??null,
      status:j?.status??p.status??core.status??null,
      percent:finite(p.percent??core.progressPercent),
      currentSamples:finite(p.currentSamples??core.currentSamples),maximumSamples:finite(p.maximumSamples??core.maximumSamples),

      engine:j?.settings?.engine??core.engine??null,
      gi:!!j?.settings?.globalIllumination,
      error:j?.error?String(j.error.message||j.error):core.error||null,errorStage:j?.error?.stage||core.errorStage||null
    };
  }

  function learn(code,meta,input={}){
    const report={schema:1,version:VERSION,code,name:meta.name,severity:input.severity||meta.severity,category:meta.category,
      at:now(),renderId:input.renderId??job()?.id??null,stage:input.stage||'render',message:String(input.message||meta.name),
      evidence:safe(input.evidence||jobEvidence()),status:input.status||'open',source:input.source||'complete-render-debugger'};
    pushEvent(report);
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NDebugRegistry?.register?.({category:meta.category,subtype:code.toLowerCase(),severity:report.severity,subsystem:'l3n-complete-render-debugger',source:report.source,message:code+' '+report.message,reproduced:true,regression:['high','critical'].includes(report.severity),evidence:report,status:report.status});}catch(_){}
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NTargetLearning?.record?.(15,'learning',code+' '+meta.name);}catch(_){}
    return Object.freeze({...report});
  }

    const all=catalog();

    'RND-1501':Object.freeze({name:'Render diagnostic data corruption',severity:'critical',category:'rendering'})
  });

  const state={events:[],samples:[],renders:{},currentId:null,lastJobStatus:null,lastProgress:null,lastProgressAt:0,lastSampleAt:0,startedAt:now()};

  function catalog(){return Object.freeze({...((base()?.codes)||{}),...EXTRA});}
  function pushEvent(e){state.events.push(e);if(state.events.length>MAX_EVENTS)state.events.shift();return e;}
  function pushSample(x){state.samples.push(x);if(state.samples.length>MAX_SAMPLES)state.samples.shift();return x;}
  function job(){return window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.activeJob||window.RenderFramework?.jobs?.lastJob||null;}
  function jobEvidence(j=job()){
    const b=base();
    const core=b?.jobEvidence?.(j)||{};
    const p=j?.progress||{};
    const ps=j?.performanceState||j?.performance||{};
    return {...safe(core),
      renderId:j?.id??core.renderId??null,
      status:j?.status??p.status??core.status??null,
      percent:finite(p.percent??core.progressPercent),
      currentSamples:finite(p.currentSamples??core.currentSamples),maximumSamples:finite(p.maximumSamples??core.maximumSamples),
      elapsedMs:finite(p.elapsedMs??core.elapsedMs),noise:finite(p.noise??core.noise),threads:finite(p.threads),
      raysPerSecond:finite(p.raysPerSecond??ps.raysPerSecond??ps.estimatedRaysPerSecond??core.raysPerSecond),
      totalRays:finite(ps.totalRays??ps.rays??ps.rayCount??core.raysCast),primaryRays:finite(ps.primaryRays??core.primaryRays),
      secondaryRays:finite(ps.secondaryRays??core.secondaryRays),shadowRays:finite(ps.shadowRays??core.shadowRays),
      reflectionRays:finite(ps.reflectionRays??core.reflectionRays),refractionRays:finite(ps.refractionRays??core.refractionRays),
      intersections:finite(ps.intersections??core.intersections),misses:finite(ps.misses??core.misses),bvhTests:finite(ps.bvhTests??core.bvhTests),
      invalidRays:finite(ps.invalidRays??core.invalidRays),bounceDepth:finite(ps.maxBounce??ps.bounceDepth??core.bounceDepth),
      resolution:Array.isArray(p.resolution)?p.resolution.slice(0,2):core.resolution||null,
      engine:j?.settings?.engine??core.engine??null,
      gi:!!j?.settings?.globalIllumination,
      error:j?.error?String(j.error.message||j.error):core.error||null,errorStage:j?.error?.stage||core.errorStage||null
    };
  }

  function learn(code,meta,input={}){
    const report={schema:1,version:VERSION,code,name:meta.name,severity:input.severity||meta.severity,category:meta.category,
      at:now(),renderId:input.renderId??job()?.id??null,stage:input.stage||'render',message:String(input.message||meta.name),
      evidence:safe(input.evidence||jobEvidence()),status:input.status||'open',source:input.source||'complete-render-debugger'};
    pushEvent(report);
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NDebugRegistry?.register?.({category:meta.category,subtype:code.toLowerCase(),severity:report.severity,subsystem:'l3n-complete-render-debugger',source:report.source,message:code+' '+report.message,reproduced:true,regression:['high','critical'].includes(report.severity),evidence:report,status:report.status});}catch(_){}
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NTargetLearning?.record?.(15,'learning',code+' '+meta.name);}catch(_){}
    return Object.freeze({...report});
  }

  function record(code,input={}){
    const all=catalog();
    if(base()?.codes?.[code]){const r=base().record(code,input);pushEvent({...safe(r),source:'base-render-debugger'});return r;}
    const meta=all[code]||EXTRA['RND-1194'];
    const actual=all[code]?code:'RND-1194';
    return learn(actual,meta,{...input,message:all[code]?input.message:('Unregistered code '+String(code)+(input.message?': '+input.message:''))});
  }

  function classify(message,stage='render'){
    const m=String(message?.message||message||'').toLowerCase();
    if(/unknown|unregistered/.test(m)&&/rnd|render/.test(m))return 'RND-1194';
    if(/uv/.test(m)&&/nan|invalid|outside|non-finite/.test(m))return 'RND-0602';
    if(/texture/.test(m)&&/sample|sampling/.test(m)&&/fail|error|invalid/.test(m))return 'RND-0603';
    if(/shader/.test(m)&&/fail|error|exception|invalid/.test(m))return 'RND-0504';
    if(/material/.test(m)&&/nan|infinity|invalid value|roughness|metalness/.test(m))return 'RND-0503';
    if(/ray direction/.test(m)&&/nan|invalid|infinity|zero/.test(m))return 'RND-0204';
    if(/ray origin/.test(m)&&/nan|invalid|infinity/.test(m))return 'RND-0205';
    if(/framebuffer/.test(m)&&/alloc|memory|create|fail/.test(m))return 'RND-1002';
    if(/canvas|image|imagedata|putimagedata/.test(m)&&/fail|error|exception/.test(m))return 'RND-1003';
    if(/scheduler|task queue|tile task/.test(m)&&/fail|error/.test(m))return 'RND-1105';
    if(/cancel/.test(m)&&/fail|stuck|error/.test(m))return 'RND-1106';
    if(/camera/.test(m)&&/mismatch|different|stale/.test(m))return 'RND-1202';
    if(/denois/.test(m)&&/nan|infinity|non-finite/.test(m))return 'RND-1302';
    if(/firefl|hot pixel|high-energy|high energy/.test(m))return 'RND-0404';
    return base()?.classify?.(message,stage)||'RND-0001';
  }

  function inspectFinite(ev){
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(ev||{})){
      if(typeof v==='number'&&!Number.isFinite(v)){record('RND-0801',{message:'Non-finite render diagnostic value: '+k,evidence:ev,stage:'diagnostics'});return false;}
    }
    return true;
  }

  function poll(){
    const j=job();if(!j)return;
    const ev=jobEvidence(j);const id=ev.renderId??'unknown';
    if(state.currentId!==id){state.currentId=id;state.lastJobStatus=null;state.lastProgress=null;state.lastProgressAt=perf();state.renders[id]={startedAt:now(),stages:[],samples:[],errors:[]};}
    const r=state.renders[id]||(state.renders[id]={startedAt:now(),stages:[],samples:[],errors:[]});
    const status=String(ev.status||'');const pct=finite(ev.percent)??0;
    if(status!==state.lastJobStatus){r.stages.push({status,at:now(),percent:pct});state.lastJobStatus=status;state.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    if(state.lastProgress===null||pct>state.lastProgress+0.0001){state.lastProgress=pct;state.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    if(perf()-state.lastSampleAt>=250){const sm={at:now(),...ev};pushSample(sm);r.samples.push(sm);if(r.samples.length>300)r.samples.shift();state.lastSampleAt=perf();inspectFinite(ev);}
    const stalled=perf()-state.lastProgressAt;
    if(/rendering/i.test(status)&&stalled>8000&&!r.stallCode){r.stallCode='RND-0101';record('RND-0101',{message:'Render stalled for '+Math.round(stalled)+' ms',evidence:ev});}
    if(/preparing|rendering/i.test(status)&&stalled>30000&&!r.timeoutCode){r.timeoutCode='RND-0103';record('RND-0103',{message:'Render stage exceeded 30 seconds without progress',evidence:ev});}
    if(ev.error&&!r.errorCaptured){r.errorCaptured=true;const code=classify(ev.error,ev.errorStage);r.errors.push({code,message:ev.error,at:now()});record(code,{message:ev.error,evidence:ev,stage:ev.errorStage||'render'});}
    if(/finished|complete/i.test(status)||j?.finished){r.finishedAt=r.finishedAt||now();r.final=ev;}
  }

  function snapshot(){
    const b=safe(base()?.snapshot?.()||null);
    return Object.freeze({schema:2,version:VERSION,codes:catalog(),base:b,events:Object.freeze(state.events.map(x=>Object.freeze({...x}))),samples:Object.freeze(state.samples.map(x=>Object.freeze({...x}))),renders:safe(state.renders),active:jobEvidence(job()),generatedAt:now()});
  }
  function summary(){
    const snap=snapshot();const counts={};for(const e of snap.events||[])if(e.code)counts[e.code]=(counts[e.code]||0)+1;
    return Object.freeze({version:VERSION,eventCount:snap.events.length,renderCount:Object.keys(snap.renders||{}).length,lastCode:snap.events.at(-1)?.code||snap.base?.lastCode||null,codeCounts:Object.freeze(counts),active:snap.active});
  }

  function mergeRuntimeReport(report){
    const out=safe(report)||{};
    out.schema=Math.max(1,Number(out.schema)||1);
    out.version=VERSION;
    out.baselineVersion=VERSION;
    out.runtimeLearningVersion=VERSION;
    out.renderDebug=snapshot();
    out.renderDebugSummary=summary();
    out.rndEvidence=(out.renderDebug.events||[]).filter(x=>x.code);
    out.l3nEvidence=Math.max(Number(out.l3nEvidence)||0,5)+out.rndEvidence.length;
    out.versionSynchronized=true;
    return out;
  }

  function catalog(){return Object.freeze({...((base()?.codes)||{}),...EXTRA});}
  function pushEvent(e){state.events.push(e);if(state.events.length>MAX_EVENTS)state.events.shift();return e;}

  function jobEvidence(j=job()){

  function learn(code,meta,input={}){
    const report={schema:1,version:VERSION,code,name:meta.name,severity:input.severity||meta.severity,category:meta.category,
      at:now(),renderId:input.renderId??job()?.id??null,stage:input.stage||'render',message:String(input.message||meta.name),
      evidence:safe(input.evidence||jobEvidence()),status:input.status||'open',source:input.source||'complete-render-debugger'};
    pushEvent(report);
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NDebugRegistry?.register?.({category:meta.category,subtype:code.toLowerCase(),severity:report.severity,subsystem:'l3n-complete-render-debugger',source:report.source,message:code+' '+report.message,reproduced:true,regression:['high','critical'].includes(report.severity),evidence:report,status:report.status});}catch(_){}
    try{window.ThreeDLiteL3NTargetLearning?.record?.(15,'learning',code+' '+meta.name);}catch(_){}
    return Object.freeze({...report});
  }

  function record(code,input={}){
    const all=catalog();
    if(base()?.codes?.[code]){const r=base().record(code,input);pushEvent({...safe(r),source:'base-render-debugger'});return r;}
    const meta=all[code]||EXTRA['RND-1194'];
    const actual=all[code]?code:'RND-1194';
    return learn(actual,meta,{...input,message:all[code]?input.message:('Unregistered code '+String(code)+(input.message?': '+input.message:''))});
  }

  function classify(message,stage='render'){
    const m=String(message?.message||message||'').toLowerCase();
    if(/unknown|unregistered/.test(m)&&/rnd|render/.test(m))return 'RND-1194';
    if(/uv/.test(m)&&/nan|invalid|outside|non-finite/.test(m))return 'RND-0602';
    if(/texture/.test(m)&&/sample|sampling/.test(m)&&/fail|error|invalid/.test(m))return 'RND-0603';
    if(/shader/.test(m)&&/fail|error|exception|invalid/.test(m))return 'RND-0504';
    if(/material/.test(m)&&/nan|infinity|invalid value|roughness|metalness/.test(m))return 'RND-0503';
    if(/ray direction/.test(m)&&/nan|invalid|infinity|zero/.test(m))return 'RND-0204';
    if(/ray origin/.test(m)&&/nan|invalid|infinity/.test(m))return 'RND-0205';
    if(/framebuffer/.test(m)&&/alloc|memory|create|fail/.test(m))return 'RND-1002';
    if(/canvas|image|imagedata|putimagedata/.test(m)&&/fail|error|exception/.test(m))return 'RND-1003';
    if(/scheduler|task queue|tile task/.test(m)&&/fail|error/.test(m))return 'RND-1105';
    if(/cancel/.test(m)&&/fail|stuck|error/.test(m))return 'RND-1106';
    if(/camera/.test(m)&&/mismatch|different|stale/.test(m))return 'RND-1202';
    if(/denois/.test(m)&&/nan|infinity|non-finite/.test(m))return 'RND-1302';
    if(/firefl|hot pixel|high-energy|high energy/.test(m))return 'RND-0404';
    return base()?.classify?.(message,stage)||'RND-0001';
  }

  function inspectFinite(ev){
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(ev||{})){
      if(typeof v==='number'&&!Number.isFinite(v)){record('RND-0801',{message:'Non-finite render diagnostic value: '+k,evidence:ev,stage:'diagnostics'});return false;}
    }
    return true;
  }

  function poll(){
    const j=job();if(!j)return;
    const ev=jobEvidence(j);const id=ev.renderId??'unknown';
    if(state.currentId!==id){state.currentId=id;state.lastJobStatus=null;state.lastProgress=null;state.lastProgressAt=perf();state.renders[id]={startedAt:now(),stages:[],samples:[],errors:[]};}
    const r=state.renders[id]||(state.renders[id]={startedAt:now(),stages:[],samples:[],errors:[]});
    const status=String(ev.status||'');const pct=finite(ev.percent)??0;
    if(status!==state.lastJobStatus){r.stages.push({status,at:now(),percent:pct});state.lastJobStatus=status;state.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    if(state.lastProgress===null||pct>state.lastProgress+0.0001){state.lastProgress=pct;state.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    if(perf()-state.lastSampleAt>=250){const sm={at:now(),...ev};pushSample(sm);r.samples.push(sm);if(r.samples.length>300)r.samples.shift();state.lastSampleAt=perf();inspectFinite(ev);}
    const stalled=perf()-state.lastProgressAt;
    if(/rendering/i.test(status)&&stalled>8000&&!r.stallCode){r.stallCode='RND-0101';record('RND-0101',{message:'Render stalled for '+Math.round(stalled)+' ms',evidence:ev});}
    if(/preparing|rendering/i.test(status)&&stalled>30000&&!r.timeoutCode){r.timeoutCode='RND-0103';record('RND-0103',{message:'Render stage exceeded 30 seconds without progress',evidence:ev});}
    if(ev.error&&!r.errorCaptured){r.errorCaptured=true;const code=classify(ev.error,ev.errorStage);r.errors.push({code,message:ev.error,at:now()});record(code,{message:ev.error,evidence:ev,stage:ev.errorStage||'render'});}
    if(/finished|complete/i.test(status)||j?.finished){r.finishedAt=r.finishedAt||now();r.final=ev;}
  }

  function snapshot(){
    const b=safe(base()?.snapshot?.()||null);
    return Object.freeze({schema:2,version:VERSION,codes:catalog(),base:b,events:Object.freeze(state.events.map(x=>Object.freeze({...x}))),samples:Object.freeze(state.samples.map(x=>Object.freeze({...x}))),renders:safe(state.renders),active:jobEvidence(job()),generatedAt:now()});
  }
  function summary(){
    const snap=snapshot();const counts={};for(const e of snap.events||[])if(e.code)counts[e.code]=(counts[e.code]||0)+1;

  }

  }

/* 3DLite v3.97.8 — selectable/copyable render-debug list in editor render window */
(()=>{
  const VERSION='3.97.8';
  const STATE={timer:null,lastText:'',opened:false};
  const safe=v=>{try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));}catch(_){return null;}};
  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/\r?\n/g,' ');
  const fmtTime=t=>{try{return new Date(t||Date.now()).toLocaleTimeString();}catch(_){return '--:--:--';}};

  function sourceSnapshot(){
    const complete=window.ThreeDLiteL3NCompleteRenderDebugger?.snapshot?.();
    const base=window.ThreeDLiteL3NRenderDebugger?.snapshot?.();
    return {complete:safe(complete),base:safe(base)};
  }

  function collectEvents(){
    const {complete,base}=sourceSnapshot();
    const rows=[];
    const seen=new Set();
    const add=e=>{
      if(!e||!e.code)return;
      const key=[e.code,e.at||e.detectedAt||'',e.renderId||'',e.message||''].join('|');
      if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);
      rows.push({
        at:e.at||e.detectedAt||Date.now(),code:e.code||'RND-1194',severity:e.severity||'info',
        renderId:e.renderId??'',stage:e.stage||'',status:e.status||'',message:e.message||e.name||'',
        evidence:e.evidence||null
      });
    };
    for(const e of complete?.events||[])add(e);
    for(const e of complete?.base?.reports||[])add(e);
    for(const e of base?.reports||[])add(e);
    rows.sort((a,b)=>(a.at||0)-(b.at||0));
    return {rows,complete,base};
  }

  function buildText(){
    const {rows,complete,base}=collectEvents();
    const active=complete?.active||base?.active||null;
    const lines=[];
    lines.push('3DLite Render Debug List v'+VERSION);
    lines.push('Errors: '+rows.length);
    if(active){
      lines.push('Active Render: '+String(active.renderId??'—')+' | '+String(active.status??'—')+' | '+String(active.percent??active.progressPercent??'—')+'%');
    }
    lines.push('');
    if(!rows.length){
      lines.push('No RND render errors recorded.');
    }else{
      rows.forEach((r,i)=>{
        lines.push(String(i+1).padStart(3,'0')+' | '+fmtTime(r.at)+' | '+esc(r.code)+' | '+String(r.severity||'').toUpperCase()+' | Render '+esc(r.renderId||'—')+' | '+esc(r.stage||'—')+' | '+esc(r.status||'—')+' | '+esc(r.message));
      });
    }
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('--- CURRENT RENDER EVIDENCE ---');
    if(active){
      const keys=['renderId','status','percent','progressPercent','currentSamples','maximumSamples','elapsedMs','resolution','raysPerSecond','totalRays','raysCast','primaryRays','secondaryRays','shadowRays','reflectionRays','refractionRays','intersections','misses','bvhTests','invalidRays','bounceDepth','noise','engine','error','errorStage'];
      for(const k of keys){
        const v=active[k]; if(v!==undefined&&v!==null&&v!=='') lines.push(k+': '+(Array.isArray(v)?v.join(' x '):String(v)));
      }
    }else lines.push('No active render.');
    return lines.join('\n');
  }

  function ensureUI(){

    if(!document.getElementById('l3nRenderDebugListWindow')){
      const panel=document.createElement('div');
      panel.id='l3nRenderDebugListWindow';

      panel.innerHTML=`
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px;font:12px sans-serif;color:#ddd;">
          <b style="margin-right:auto">Render Debugging List <span style="font-weight:normal;color:#aaa">v${VERSION}</span></b>
          <button type="button" id="l3nRenderDebugRefresh">Refresh</button>
          <button type="button" id="l3nRenderDebugCopySelected">Copy Selected</button>
          <button type="button" id="l3nRenderDebugCopyAll">Copy All</button>
          <button type="button" id="l3nRenderDebugClose">Close</button>
        </div>
        <textarea id="l3nRenderDebugListText" readonly spellcheck="false" aria-label="Render debugging list" style="width:100%;height:calc(100% - 32px);resize:none;background:#111;color:#ddd;border:1px solid #444;padding:8px;font:11px/1.45 Consolas,monospace;white-space:pre;overflow:auto;user-select:text;-webkit-user-select:text;cursor:text;box-sizing:border-box;"></textarea>
        <div id="l3nRenderDebugCopyStatus" style="position:absolute;left:10px;bottom:3px;font:10px sans-serif;color:#aaa;pointer-events:none"></div>`;
      win.appendChild(panel);

      panel.querySelector('#l3nRenderDebugCopyAll')?.addEventListener('click',copyAll);

    }
    return true;
  }

  function status(msg){const el=document.getElementById('l3nRenderDebugCopyStatus');if(el){el.textContent=msg;setTimeout(()=>{if(el.textContent===msg)el.textContent='';},1400);}}
  function refresh(){
    if(!ensureUI())return '';

    const text=buildText();STATE.lastText=text;
    if(ta&&ta.value!==text){const a=ta.selectionStart,b=ta.selectionEnd;ta.value=text;try{ta.setSelectionRange(Math.min(a,text.length),Math.min(b,text.length));}catch(_){}}

  }
  async function writeClipboard(text){
    if(!text)return false;
    try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);return true;}catch(_){
      const ta=document.getElementById('l3nRenderDebugListText');if(!ta)return false;ta.focus();document.execCommand?.('copy');return true;
    }
  }

  }
  function open(){if(!ensureUI())return false;const p=document.getElementById('l3nRenderDebugListWindow');p.style.display='block';STATE.opened=true;refresh();startVisibleTimer();document.getElementById('l3nRenderDebugListText')?.focus();return true;}
  function close(){const p=document.getElementById('l3nRenderDebugListWindow');if(p)p.style.display='none';STATE.opened=false;stopVisibleTimer();return true;}
  function toggle(){return STATE.opened?close():open();}

      const beauty=[accumulator.sum[o]/s,accumulator.sum[o+1]/s,accumulator.sum[o+2]/s];
      aovs.set3('Beauty',i,beauty);
      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');
      const direct=d?[d[o],d[o+1],d[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      const emission=e?[e[o],e[o+1],e[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      aovs.set3('Global Illumination',i,[
        Math.max(0,beauty[0]-direct[0]-emission[0]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[1]-direct[1]-emission[1]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[2]-direct[2]-emission[2])
      ]);
    }
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
        samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
        passes:completedPasses,
        maxBounces:PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job),
        estimator:'Path GI + direct NEE + power-heuristic MIS',
        mis:true,
        adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
        progressive:!!settings.progressive,
        russianRoulette:true,
        importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture',
        noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
        transmission:true,
        secondaryGI:settings.secondaryGI,
        lightCache:job.lightCache?.stats?.()||null,
        emissiveTriangleLights:compiled.emissiveTriangles?.triangles?.length||0,
        diagnostics:RenderPart5Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  }catch(e){errors.push('Sampling settings test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

class RenderSettingsModel{
  constructor(){
    this.engine='fast';
    this.quality='Preview';
    this.width=1280;this.height=720;this.percentage=100;
    this.aspectLock=true;this.transparent=false;
    this.format='PNG';this.colorDepth='8-bit';
    this.camera='Active View';this.focalLength=50;this.sensorWidth=36;
    this.fStop=8;this.iso=100;
    this.globalIllumination=true;this.primaryGI='Brute Force';this.secondaryGI='Brute Force';
    this.progressive=true;this.adaptive=true;this.minSamples=8;this.maxSamples=64;this.noiseThreshold=.03;
    this.maxBounces=5;this.denoise='Off';this.toneMapping='ACES';this.exposure=0;
    this.background='#202020';this.environmentStrength=1;
    this.cpuThreads='Auto';this.tileSize='Auto';this.acceleration='BVH';
    this.lightTree=false;this.pathGuiding=false;
  }
  clone(){return Object.freeze({...this});}
}

class RenderProgressState{
  constructor(){
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.percent=0;this.currentSamples=0;this.maximumSamples=0;
    this.noise=null;this.threads=0;this.resolution=[0,0];this.gi='—';
    this.elapsedMs=0;this.raysPerSecond=null;this.bvhBuildMs=null;
  }
  snapshot(){return Object.freeze({...this,resolution:Object.freeze([...this.resolution])});}
}

class RenderJob{
  constructor(id,settings,mode='full',region=null){
    this.id=id;
    this.settings=settings;
    this.mode=mode;
    this.region=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.cancelled=false;this.paused=false;this.finished=false;this.error=null;
    this.startedAt=0;this.finishedAt=0;this.renderStartedAt=0;
    this.progress=new RenderProgressState();
    this.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    this.controlToken16=new RenderControlToken16();
    this.setCancelled=v=>{this.cancelled=!!v;if(v)this.controlToken16.cancel();};
    this.setPaused=v=>{this.paused=!!v;v?this.controlToken16.pause():this.controlToken16.resume();};
  }
}

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

var RenderPart2Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.16-part2-core',
  validateSnapshot(scene){
    const r=RenderSnapshotValidator.validate(scene);if(!r.ok)throw new SnapshotError(r.errors.join('; '));return scene;
  },
  compile(scene){this.validateSnapshot(scene);return RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(scene);},
  buildAcceleration(scene){
    const packets=this.compile(scene);const store=new RenderTriangleStore();packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));store.finalize();
    return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
  },
  ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height){return RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,px,py,width,height);},
  diagnostics(acceleration=null){return Object.freeze({geometry:RenderGeometryCompiler.lastStats,acceleration:acceleration?.stats||null});},
  validate(){
    const errors=[];const g2=validateRenderGroup2(),g3=validateRenderGroup3(),g4=validateRenderGroup4(),p=runRenderPart2SelfTests();
    if(!g2.ok)errors.push(...g2.errors);if(!g3.ok)errors.push(...g3.errors);if(!g4.ok)errors.push(...g4.errors);if(!p.ok)errors.push(...p.errors);
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderPart2Core=RenderPart2Core;

const RenderCore1=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-1',
  createRequest(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    try{
      const job=RenderCoreA.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RendererRuntimeState.snapshotReady=!!job?.renderScene;
      return job;
    }catch(err){
      RendererRuntimeState.snapshotReady=false;
      throw err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);
    }
  },
  createExecution(job){
    if(!job?.renderScene)throw new SnapshotError('Missing immutable RenderSceneSnapshot');
    try{return {job,geometry:RenderCoreB.createExecutionGeometry(job.renderScene)};}
    catch(err){throw err instanceof RendererError?err:new GeometryCompileError(String(err?.message||err),err);}
  },
  async prepareExecution(execution,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    try{execution.prepared=await RenderCoreB.prepareWorkerGeometry(execution.job,pool,execution.geometry,onProgress);return execution;}
    catch(err){throw err instanceof RendererError?err:new WorkerError(String(err?.message||err),err);}
  },
  buildAcceleration(execution){
    try{execution.acceleration=RenderCoreB.buildAccelerationFromPackets(execution.geometry.accelerationPackets);RendererRuntimeState.accelerationReady=true;return execution;}
    catch(err){RendererRuntimeState.accelerationReady=false;throw new BVHError(String(err?.message||err),err);}
  },
  validate(){const errors=[];const a=RenderCoreA.validate(),b=RenderCoreB.validate(),p3=validateRenderPart3();if(!a.ok)errors.push(...a.errors);if(!b.ok)errors.push(...b.errors);if(!p3.ok)errors.push(...p3.errors);return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};}
});
const RenderCore2=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-2',
  compileShading(job){try{const c=RenderCoreC.compileScene(job.renderScene,job.settings);return Object.freeze({materials:c.materials,lights:c.lights,settings:job.settings,scene:job.renderScene});}catch(err){throw new MaterialError(String(err?.message||err),err);}},
  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){return RenderCoreD.evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);},
  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){return RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection);},
  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){return RenderCoreD.tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit);},
  validate(){const errors=[];const c=RenderCoreC.validate(),d=RenderCoreD.validate(),p4=validateRenderPart4(),p5=validateRenderPart5();if(!c.ok)errors.push(...c.errors);if(!d.ok)errors.push(...d.errors);if(!p4.ok)errors.push(...p4.errors);if(!p5.ok)errors.push(...p5.errors);return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};}
});
const RendererMasterCore=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-master-core',
  createRequest(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    return RenderCore1.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
  },
  async prepare(job,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    let t=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    let e=RenderCore1.createExecution(job);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'geometry',t);

    t=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    e=await RenderCore1.prepareExecution(e,pool,onProgress);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'worker',t);

    t=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    e=RenderCore1.buildAcceleration(e);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'bvh',t);

    t=performance.now();
    const baseShading=RenderCore2.compileShading(job);
    e.shading=RenderCoreE.prepareShading(job,e.acceleration,baseShading);
    RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'shading',t);
    return e;
  },
  rendererFor(job){return RenderCoreE.rendererFor(job);},
  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const a=RenderCore1.validate(),b=RenderCore2.validate(),e=RenderCoreE.validate(),p6=validateRenderPart6();
    if(!a.ok)errors.push(...a.errors);
    if(!b.ok)errors.push(...b.errors);
    if(!e.ok)errors.push(...e.errors);
    if(!p6.ok)errors.push(...p6.errors);
    if(typeof RenderCore1.createRequest!=='function')errors.push('Core 1 request API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCore1.createExecution!=='function')errors.push('Core 1 execution API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCore2.compileShading!=='function')errors.push('Core 2 shading API missing');
    if(typeof RenderCoreE.prepareShading!=='function'||typeof RenderCoreE.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Core E API missing');
    if(typeof this.prepare!=='function'||typeof this.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Master Core orchestration API missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderCore1,RenderCore2,RenderCoreE,RendererMasterCore});
var RenderPerformanceMetrics=Object.freeze({
  begin(job){job.startedAt=performance.now();job.finishedAt=0;return job.startedAt;},
  beginTracing(job){job.renderStartedAt=performance.now();},
  finish(job){
    job.finishedAt=performance.now();
    job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,job.finishedAt-(job.startedAt||job.finishedAt));
    const seconds=Math.max(1e-6,(job.finishedAt-(job.renderStartedAt||job.startedAt||job.finishedAt))/1000);
    const measured=RenderCoreH.performance(job);
    const actualRays=Math.max(0,Number(measured?.totalRays)||0);
    job.progress.raysPerSecond=actualRays?Math.round(actualRays/seconds):0;
    job.performance=Object.freeze({
      totalMs:job.progress.elapsedMs,
      traceMs:seconds*1000,
      actualRays,
      raysPerSecond:job.progress.raysPerSecond,
      samples:Number(measured?.samples)||0,
      yields:Number(measured?.yields)||0,
      bvhBuildMs:job.progress.bvhBuildMs
    });
    return job.performance;
  }
});

class RenderJobController{
  constructor(onProgress){
    this.activeJob=null;
    this.last
--- occurrence 4 at 487572 ---
eneration++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
    }else if(region){
      region=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!region)return false;
    }
    if(RendererRuntimeState.degraded){
      const criticalReady=
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.createRequest==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.prepare==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.rendererFor==='function' &&
        !!this.jobs &&
        !!this.ui?.frameBuffer;

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
        RendererBootstrap.showFailure(job.error);
        return job;
      }
    }
    try{
      const snapshotStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
      job=RendererMasterCore.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      RenderPerformanceRuntime16.markStage(job,'snapshot',snapshotStart16);
      this.jobs?.configureThreads?.(job.settings.cpuThreads);
    }catch(err){
      job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'snapshot',job.error);
      this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice)notice.innerHTML='Render snapshot failed.<br>'+String(err?.message||err);
      console.error('3DLite Render snapshot:',err);
      return job;
    }
    this.lastSettings=job.settings;
    this.lastRegion=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.resize(...job.progress.resolution);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice)notice.innerHTML=`Render snapshot ready.<br>${job.renderScene.objects.length} object(s) • ${job.renderScene.materials.length} material(s).`;
    this.jobs.start(job);
    return job;
  },

  renderLast(){
    const settings=this.lastCompletedSettings||this.lastSettings;
    const region=this.lastCompletedSettings?this.lastCompletedRegion:this.lastRegion;
    return settings?this.render('last',region,settings):this.render();
  },

  mapDisplayedRegion(localRegion){
    return RenderRegion14.mapNested(this.lastCompletedRegion,localRegion);
  },

  renderRegion(region=null){
    if(region){
      const clean=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!clean)return false;
      this.pendingRegion=clean;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
      return this.render('region',clean);
    }

    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    if(this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame){
      this.regionSelectArmed=true;
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        notice.style.display='flex';
        notice.innerHTML='Render Region: drag on the render image to choose an area.';
      }
      return true;
    }

    const fallback=Object.freeze({x:.25,y:.25,width:.5,height:.5});
    this.pendingRegion=fallback;
    return this.render('region',fallback);
  },

  recordCompleted(job){
    if(!job||job.cancelled||job.error)return false;
    this.lastCompletedSettings=job.settings||null;
    this.lastCompletedRegion=RenderRegion14.sanitize(job.region);
    return true;
  },

  stop(){return this.jobs.cancel(true);},
  pause(){return this.jobs.pause();},
  resume(){return this.jobs.resume();},
  restart(){
    const settings=this.lastSettings||this.readSettings();
    return this.render('restart',this.lastRegion,settings);
  },

  showSettings(){this.ui.open(this.ui.settingsWindow);},
  showFrameBuffer(){this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();this.updateFrameActions();},

  updateFrameActions(){
    const valid=!!this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame;
    for(const id of ['rfSave','rfCopy']){
      const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.disabled=!valid;
    }
    return valid;
  },

  showChannel(name){
    const job=this.jobs?.activeJob||this.jobs?.lastJob;
    let frame=null;
    try{
      frame=RenderPresenter13.frame(job,name);
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('[3DLite Group13] Presentation failed:',err);
      frame=name==='Beauty'?job?.beautyFrame||null:null;
    }
    if(!frame)return false;
    this.ui.frameBuffer.channel=name;
    this.ui.frameBuffer.present(frame);
    this.updateFrameActions();
    return true;
  },

  clearFrame(){
    this.ui.frameBuffer.clear();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.textContent='No rendered frame.';}
    this.updateFrameActions?.();
  },

  saveFrame(){
    const fb=this.ui.frameBuffer;
    if(!fb?.hasValidFrame)return false;
    const requested=(this.lastCompletedSettings?.format||this.lastSettings?.format||this.readSettings().format||'PNG').toUpperCase();
    const mime=requested==='JPEG'?'image/jpeg':'image/png';
    const ext=requested==='JPEG'?'jpg':'png';
    const channel=String(fb.channel||document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,'_');
    const region=this.lastCompletedRegion?'_Region':'';
    const a=document.createElement('a');
    a.download=`3DLite_Render${region}_${channel}.${ext}`;
    a.href=fb.canvas.toDataURL(mime,requested==='JPEG'?.94:undefined);
    a.click();
    return true;
  },

  async copyFrame(){
    try{
      if(!this.ui.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame)return false;
      const blob=await new Promise(r=>this.ui.frameBuffer.canvas.toBlob(r,'image/png'));
      if(!blob||!navigator.clipboard?.write||typeof ClipboardItem==='undefined')return false;
      await navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({'image/png':blob})]);
      return true;
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('3DLite render copy failed:',err);
      return false;
    }
  }
};

const RenderSettingsPolicy=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(s){
    const x={...s};
    x.engine=x.engine==='path'?'path':'fast';
    x.quality=['Draft','Preview','High','Ultra','Custom'].includes(x.quality)?x.quality:'Preview';
    x.width=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.width)||1280)));
    x.height=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.height)||720)));
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Math.min(200,Number(x.percentage)||100));
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.maxBounces)||5));
    x.background=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(x.background||'')?x.background:'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Math.min(1e6,Number(x.environmentStrength)||0));
    x.cpuThreads=x.cpuThreads==='Auto'?'Auto':String(Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.cpuThreads)||1)));
    x.tileSize=String(x.tileSize||'

ium',category:'lighting'}),

    'RND-0901':Object.freeze({name:'Render pass failure',severity:'high',category:'rendering'}),
    'RND-0902':Object.freeze({name:'Pass compositing failure',severity:'high',category:'rendering'}),

  const state={version:VERSION,reports:[],active:null,last:null,lastCode:null,monitor:null};
  const safe=v=>{try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));}catch(_){return null;}};
  const num=v=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):null;
  const perf=()=>performance.now();
